SRB / memo trap #4). Publish or leak the Fork-A source later and the link can snap back — hence the forward-looking re-test duty. |\n| TM5 | Re-identification adversary (outside project, no key needed) | Run same deterministic detector on public videos, correlate-match a stream to its source | NOT achievable against the committed subset because: (a) source is Fork-A/unpublished (no permanent cheap public reference); (b) fixtures minimized to ~5 invariant scalars/window + relative intervals, far below the raw per-frame (x,y) that makes trajectory-linkage work — the raw CSV is NEVER committed; (c) absolute timestamps reset to a relative origin. | Becomes LIVE if a Fork-A source is ever published — exactly why provenance gating + the 'data-environment grows' re-assessment (NIST SP 800-188 / ICO motivated-intruder / OVIC) are load-bearing, not optional. |\n| TM6 | Accidental plaintext commit (most likely real-world failure) | A maintainer commits a decrypted *_golden_features.json or a file carrying name/video_id/match metadata | Pre-commit hook + CI scan reject any tracked file matching the private-features pattern, any 32-hex MD5 / known-player token / non-surrogate name field, and any SOPS file committed in plaintext (unencrypted-MAC check). | If the guard is bypassed/disabled, the leak lands. The guard is the mitigation; CI re-scan on every PR is the backstop. |",
1156	      "protobufSchemaSketch": "// backend/eval/fixtures/rally_fixture.proto  (proto3, package rally.fixture.v1)\n// OPTIONAL layer (Phase 4) — only if cross-OS byte-stability + characterization snapshots are wanted.\n// Serializes EXACTLY today's *_golden_features.json contract (fusion_golden.extract_video) plus\n// version guards, a de-attributed opaque id, and an expected-output snapshot + quality floor.\n// NO field exists for pose/skeleton/gait/face/bbox-track — unrepresentable BY DESIGN.\n\nsyntax = \"proto3\";\npackage rally.fixture.v1;\n\nmessage RallyFixture {\n  // --- versioning / incommensurability guards (mirror regression.py + ablation.py) ---\n  uint32 schema_version = 1;   // bump on a non-additive change; gates the loader migration shim\n  string label_version  = 2;   // Roora guideline version (e.g. \"v8\")\n  string detector_tag   = 3;   // e.g. \"wasb_hybrid@<ver>\" — the regression.py detector key\n  string gt_hash        = 4;   // regression.gt_hash(gts), recomputed + verified on load\n  float  iou_threshold  = 5;   // IoU the snapshot/floor were computed at (default 0.5)\n\n  // --- de-attribution: NO source filename / video_id MD5 / match / player ---\n  string fixture_id = 6;       // OPAQUE salted surrogate (HMAC-derived hex). NEVER the MD5 video_id.\n\n  // --- replay inputs (today's contract; time-origin reset already applied) ---\n  float fps         = 7;       // non-identifying scale constant\n  float frame_width = 8;       // non-identifying scale constant (quantize if counsel flags)\n  repeated Candidate candidates = 9;\n  repeated Interval  gts        = 10;\n\n  // --- expected outputs for the two assertions ---\n  Snapshot     expected = 11;  // blessed output of replaying this fixture (characterization)\n  QualityFloor floor    = 12;  // minimum P/R/F1 vs gts this fixture must hold\n}\n\nmessage Candidate {\n  Interval window = 1;                 // {start,end} seconds, relative to the per-fixture origin\n  // map (not packed array) so a NEW column is additive and an OLD fixture missing it defaults to\n  // 0.0 — exactly experiment._feature_column / ablation.load_videos tolerate today.\n  map<string, double> shuttle = 2;     // duration,peak_velocity,mean_velocity,active_ratio,net_crossings\n  map<string, double> person  = 3;     // TIER-1 ONLY — omitted entirely from the Tier-0 public subset\n  map<string, double> audio   = 4;     // TIER-1 ONLY — omitted from Tier-0\n  int32 label = 5;                     // IoU-derived 0/1 (training.label_candidates), kept for parity\n  // NOTE: NO pose/skeleton/gait/face/bbox column exists in this schema by design.\n}\n\nmessage Interval     { double start = 1; double end = 2; }\nmessage Snapshot     { string variant_spec_hash = 1; repeated Interval segments = 2; }  // sorted, quantized\nmessage QualityFloor { float min_precision = 1; float min_recall = 2; float min_f1 = 3; }",
1157	      "serializationDeterminism": "Cross-OS byte-stability is enforced at four layers so a fixture and its snapshot are byte-identical on Windows/Linux/mac and CI cannot flake on float repr or CRLF: (1) FLOATS — quantize every coordinate/feature before serialization: round(feature, 6), round(seconds, 3) (matching regression.gt_hash's round(.,3) on line 39); store the quantized values so platform float repr can never diverge. (2) FIELD/MAP ORDER — protobuf map iteration order is unspecified, so serialize through a canonical writer that sets deterministic_serialization on the message AND sorts candidates by (start,end), feature-map keys lexicographically, and snapshot segments by (start,end). (3) NEWLINES — fixtures are binary .pb (no newline issue); commit with `*.pb binary -text` in .gitattributes so git never CRLF-mangles them; any sidecar text/JSON debug dump is normalized to LF. (4) ROUND-TRIP — a test asserts serialize(parse(bytes)) == bytes for every committed fixture (canonical bytes are stable). If the project stays on JSON instead of protobuf, the SAME discipline applies via json.dump(..., sort_keys=True) + pre-quantized floats + LF — the existing committed JSON (eval_baselines/*) already uses sort_keys=True (regression.py:98, save_baseline). The time-origin reset is itself a determinism aid: it removes large absolute timestamps whose low-order float bits are the most platform-sensitive.",
1158	      "encryptionMechanism": "Encryption is a NARROW, honestly-scoped layer used ONLY against the no-key public (TM1) — it is belt, not parachute; the upstream minimization does the real work. RECOMMENDATION: SOPS + age for any encrypted-in-repo Tier-1 secrets (per-recipient public-key encryption, git-diff-friendly value-level for JSON, trivial CI/contributor key handling, an explicit decrypt step so there is no silent-plaintext-commit failure mode, and a MAC that detects tampering). age over GPG for ergonomics (no keyring/agent ceremony). REJECTED alternatives: git-crypt (one shared symmetric key → painful rotation, opaque binary diffs, a misconfigured checkout silently commits plaintext); git-lfs+encryption (LFS solves large-binary storage not secrecy — our feature JSONs are KB-scale, so it buys nothing and reinvents git-crypt with a server dependency). PREFERRED OVERALL for Tier-1: OUT-OF-REPO + token (the existing OneDrive store) — keeps ZERO sensitive bytes in immutable git history (a future key compromise can't decrypt old commits, and the legal 'redistribution into a public repo' act never happens for sensitive data) and reuses the #175 RALLY_GOLDEN_DIR seam. Pick per-fixture: Fork-A-clean-and-stable → in-repo SOPS is fine; anything Fork-B-tainted or identity-adjacent → out-of-repo only. CRYPTO LIBRARY note: the surrogate-id HMAC-SHA256 uses Python's stdlib hmac/hashlib (no new dependency, license-clean); SOPS+age are external CLI tools invoked from a make target, not Python deps. DOCUMENT EXPLICITLY in code comments + docs/GOLDEN_DATA_SHARING.md that Tier-1 crypto defends ONLY against the no-key public and that a running contributor necessarily holds plaintext+key — stating this plainly heads off the DPDP 'reasonable security safeguards'/misrepresentation question the memo flags.",
1159	      "keyManagement": "TWO secrets, by Tier-1 delivery mode. (A) OUT-OF-REPO+token (default): a scoped OneDrive read token. CI = repository secret RALLY_GOLDEN_TOKEN, exposed only to the Tier-1 job on trusted branches, NEVER to fork-PR runners (a malicious fork PR cannot exfiltrate it). Contributor onboarding = granted scoped read access to the private store. Rotation = revoke the share / rotate the token. (B) Encrypted-in-repo (SOPS+age): an age keypair per recipient. CI = the age PRIVATE key as repository secret RALLY_AGE_KEY, Tier-1 job only, never to fork runners. Contributor onboarding = the contributor runs age-keygen and sends their PUBLIC key; a maintainer adds it to .sops.yaml and runs `sops updatekeys` — NO existing secret is reshared, each recipient decrypts with their own private key. Rotation = edit .sops.yaml recipients (remove a departed/compromised key, add the new) + `sops updatekeys`; because age is per-recipient, removing a recipient is a one-command re-key (vs git-crypt's whole-repo re-encrypt where the old key still decrypts history). THE SALT (separate from the encryption key, and more important): the per-release HMAC salt that makes fixture_id non-reversible lives with the PRIVATE Fork-A corpus on OneDrive, NEVER in git, rotated per public release so a single salt leak does not retro-link all releases. On a suspected DATA leak: rotate the token/re-key AND — because non-attributability is revocable — re-assess whether the underlying Fork-A source must stay unpublished. A PRIVATE surrogate→source map is kept in the out-of-repo store for owner/CI debugging and is NEVER committed.",
1160	      "ciIntegration": "TWO CI jobs with a hard split so coverage never depends on a secret: (1) TIER-0 job — runs on EVERY PR including fork PRs, no key, executes tests/test_fixtures_tier0.py + the unconditional fixture-replay test against eval_baselines/fixtures/ (folds into the existing run_all_tests.py / pytest, no new runner). This is the public always-green gate. (2) TIER-1 job — gated on the secret being present (RALLY_GOLDEN_TOKEN or RALLY_AGE_KEY), runs only on trusted branches (never fork-PR runners), fetches/decrypts the corpus to a gitignored path, runs the full ΔF1/CI/sign-test + the exact Gate-A litmus. When the secret is absent the Tier-1 tests SKIP cleanly via _tier1_present() (generalizing tests/test_ablation.py:366-371). GUARD JOBS on every PR: (a) the leak scan — reject any committed 32-hex MD5 / known-player token / non-surrogate name field / private-features filename / plaintext-committed SOPS file; (b) if protobuf is adopted, re-run `protoc` and `git diff --exit-code` to prove the checked-in _gen/ module matches the .proto. GREEN with no key, no video, no DB. (4) The Tier-1 tests SKIP cleanly with a clear reason ('Tier-1 golden corpus absent: set RALLY_GOLDEN_DIR or provide the key'). # Golden Regression Fixtures — video-free, non-attributable regression suite

> **Status:** `DRAFT` — owner-gated · **Owner:** Avi · **Created:** 2026-06-16 · **Last updated:** 2026-06-16
> **Lifecycle:** `DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived` (move to `docs/archives/` when DONE, per archive policy)
> **Tracking anchors:** this doc's §7 progress tracker is the source of truth; pointer lives in `docs/NEXT_STEPS.md` + memory `current-state`.
> **Relation to existing docs:** EXTENDS (does not supersede) `docs/GOLDEN_DATA_SHARING.md` (#175); reuses the eval stack documented in `docs/EVALUATION.md` / `docs/EXPERIMENT_HARNESS.md`; quality floor ties to `docs/QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md`.
> **Honesty note:** §3 (Legal) is **researched general information, NOT legal advice** — it gates on retained counsel (§8). §2/§4/§7 mark which claims are **[verified]** against code vs **[design]** proposals.

---

## 0. TL;DR

Let a contributor on **another machine with no video, GPU, WSL, or DB** run the rally-segmentation regression suite from **committed structured files**, so refactors and non-quality PRs get a red/green side-effect signal. Two layers, built once and grown by accretion:

1. **Mechanics** — freeze the deterministic pipeline at the **post-detector trajectory seam** and replay it through the *already-shipped* eval stack (`experiment.py` / `metrics.py` / `windowing.py` / `regression.py`), with two auto-discovered gates: a **characterization/snapshot** gate (catches any behaviour change) and a **quality-floor** gate (catches quality drops).
2. **Privacy/legal filter (SEALED-FIXTURES)** — only **de-attributed, minimized, owner-source, biometric-free** numbers are ever committed; richer/sensitive streams stay key-gated or out-of-repo. Non-attributability comes from **deletion-at-source**, not encryption.

**The convergence that makes this clean:** the *privacy* answer (publish shuttle-only, drop person/pose streams) and the *determinism* answer (freeze only the pure-stdlib `CONTROL` path, keep the numpy/BLAS learned scorer off the cross-machine gate) point at the **same minimal public tier**. That coincidence is the spine of the design.

**Two findings already actionable, independent of the rest:**
- 🔴 **Live de-attribution bug** *(verified)*: real player/venue names are committed today in `eval_baselines/heuristic_lovo_n6.json`, `eval_baselines/gemini_wrapper_2026-06-10.json`, and `backend/eval/eval_partial_labels.py` (the last also has a personal `C:/Users/avidu/...` path) — and in git history. → **Deliverable P0.**
- 🟠 **Determinism trap** *(verified, corroborated by two independent red-teams)*: the learned variants (`shuttle_only`, `fusion`) train a numpy/OpenBLAS `DYNAMIC_ARCH` logistic regression whose decisions flip near the 0.5 threshold across CPUs → would false-RED on a contributor's machine. → the cross-machine gate must be **CONTROL/static-only**.

---

## 1. Problem & goal

The rally-quality measurement is split across two machines by capability (`docs/GOLDEN_DATA_SHARING.md`):
- `backend/eval/fusion_golden.py` **extracts** `*_golden_features.json` — needs the **video + trajectory CSV + labels + GPU/WSL** (GPU box only).
- `backend/eval/fusion_compare.py` / `backend/eval/ablation.py` **re-score** those JSONs on **pure CPU, no video** — any machine.

The CPU re-scoring is *already* video-free, but the inputs live in gitignored `output/` / private OneDrive, so the regression tests that depend on them **skip everywhere** (`tests/test_ablation.py::test_litmus_reproduces_gate_a`, `tests/test_fusion_compare.py`). The committed `eval_baselines/` baselines exist but `regression.py` still scores DB-stored predictions (needs a pipeline run on the video).

**Goal:** commit a tiny, version-tagged, **numeric-only, non-attributable** per-fixture corpus + auto-discovered gates so a clean `git clone` / CI gets a real red/green regression signal with **zero video/GPU/DB**, while never leaking attributable or sensitive data.

---

## 2. Decisions locked

| # | Decision | Source | Implication |
|---|----------|--------|-------------|
| D1 | **Corpus is owner-recorded / owned** | owner answer 2026-06-16 | Legal analysis takes **Fork A** (clean): no third-party copyright/ToS infringement; concern is trade-secret/moat + privacy. Strongest non-attributability (no public reference to correlate against). |
| D2 | **Repo private now, may open-source later** | owner answer 2026-06-16 | Design for **public-grade hygiene from day one** — git history is permanent; nothing scrubable-later. |
| D3 | **Adults, consent not formalized** | owner answer 2026-06-16 | **Person/pose/gait streams stay OUT of the public tier** (DPDP/GDPR derived-personal-data risk without consent). Public tier = **shuttle + abstract intervals + non-biometric features only**. |
| D4 | **Freeze seam = post-detector trajectory** *(design)* | workflow-1 synthesis | Widest deterministic surface; reuse the shipped eval stack verbatim; the raw per-frame CSV is **never committed** (only ~5 aggregate, fps/res-invariant shuttle scalars/window). |
| D5 | **Cross-machine gate is CONTROL/static-only** *(design)* | both red-teams [verified cause] | Learned variants (numpy/BLAS) are **owner-box/Tier-1 only**, asserted with tolerance, never as a cross-machine byte-snapshot. |
| D6 | **Non-attributability = deletion-at-source, encryption = public-only barrier** *(design)* | workflow-2 synthesis | A contributor who runs the tests necessarily reads plaintext+key (DRM/analog-hole). So we **delete** attribution, not hide it. Encryption only stops the no-key public. |
| D7 | **Two tiers** *(design)* | workflow-2 synthesis | **Tier-0** public/no-key (shuttle-only, de-attributed). **Tier-1** key-gated/out-of-repo (person/audio, full corpus), **skips cleanly when absent**. |

---

## 3. Legal foundation (researched — gates on counsel, §8)

**Core verdict (settled across IN/US/EU):** the derived numbers — per-frame shuttle (x,y), rally start/end intervals, audio/court/aggregate-person feature values — **do NOT inherit the source video's copyright.** They are uncopyrightable *facts/measurements*, not authored expression, and not a §101 "derivative work."

| Jurisdiction | Copyright of the data | Database right | Contract / ToS | Net |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **India** (primary) | Not copyrightable as facts (*EBC v. D.B. Modak*); but compilations get thin protection on a low "skill/labour" bar (*Burlington*, *OLX v. Padawan*) — only bites if **lifted from a 3rd-party dataset**, not self-derived | **None** (no sui-generis right) — a real permissiveness edge | Anti-scraping ToS (untested in court) + **IT Act s.43/s.66** civil/criminal exposure for 3rd-party ingestion | **Owned-source + minors-excluded + no person/pose = clean** |
| **US** | Not copyrightable (*Feist*; §102(b); *NBA v. Motorola*) | None (*Feist* killed sweat-of-the-brow) | **Dominant risk.** ToS enforceable where copyright/CFAA fail (*ProCD*, *hiQ* → injunction + **forced deletion**). Remedy for redistribution is takedown, not nominal damages | Publishable as facts **iff** owned/permissioned source + no barring ToS + no person/biometric/minor stream |
| **EU / UK** | Not copyrightable (*Football Dataco v. Yahoo*) | **Genuinely contestable.** *Football Dataco v. Sportradar*: recording pre-existing on-court facts = "**obtaining**", not "creating" → our "we created it" premise is **overconfident**; harm test = *CV-Online Latvia* | Ryanair v. PR Aviation: ToS can ban extraction/redistribution even with no IP right | Get EU+UK advice before redistributing numeric streams at scale |

**Provenance is the decisive fork.** **Fork A (owner-recorded, unpublished)** — the only path safe to commit publicly; collapses to a trade-secret *choice* (Indian breach-of-confidence / Contract Act 1872 / IT Act s.72 — **not** US DTSA) + privacy. **Fork B (broadcast/YouTube/scraped)** — do **not** commit; the extraction act needs a license or now-**contested** US fair use (*Thomson Reuters v. Ross* 2025; USCO May-2025), and unlawful acquisition independently sinks it (*Bartz v. Anthropic*, ~$1.5B settlement). Caveat: owner footage **shot at a third party's event** (tournament/club) carries ticket/venue/organiser/broadcaster terms → treat as Fork B until cleared.

**Publish-by-stream (privacy):**
- ✅ **Publish-OK:** shuttle (x,y)+Visibility (inanimate object), abstract rally/GT **intervals** (identity-stripped), non-biometric audio/court/aggregate-person features detached from identity.
- ⚠️ **Conditional (effectively never in v1):** person bounding boxes that *persistently single out a player* → flips to GDPR Art.9 the moment it identifies. This architecture is prone to that flip — treat per-player tracking as a tripwire.
- ⛔ **Never publish:** pose/skeleton/**gait** (re-identifies at 80–95% even with faces blurred — *Weiss et al. 2025*), face crops, any named-player↔movement mapping. Matches the existing "gait/biometrics strictly future (GDPR Art.9)" guardrail.
- 🚸 **Minors flip everything:** DPDP "child" = under 18; s.9(3) **prohibits behavioural monitoring** (the Rule-12 exemptions don't cover this project). A persistent per-player signature on a minor is, conservatively, prohibited. **Exclude minors** from any persisted stream beyond inanimate shuttle/interval data.

**Non-attributability is relative, not absolute.** A per-video trajectory is a fingerprint (de Montjoye: 4 points → 95% re-ID). It's only non-attributable because the **Fork-A source stays unpublished** (no public reference to correlate against). Per *EDPS v. SRB* (CJEU C-413/23 P, 2025) it **remains personal data to the holder** who can re-run the detector. So: conditional, revocable (publish the source and the link snaps back), forward-looking (re-assess as the public-video environment grows). Never market it as "anonymous."

*Key authorities:* Feist 499 U.S. 340; NBA v. Motorola 105 F.3d 841; 17 U.S.C. §101/§102(b); Sega v. Accolade 977 F.2d 1510; Football Dataco v. Yahoo (C-604/10) & v. Sportradar (C-173/11); BHB v. William Hill (C-203/02); CV-Online Latvia (C-762/19); Ryanair v. PR Aviation (C-30/14); Thomson Reuters v. Ross (D. Del. 2025); EBC v. D.B. Modak; DPDP Act 2023 s.3/s.9 + Rules 2025; EDPS v. SRB (C-413/23 P). Full URLs in the research artifact (§10).

---

## 4. Architecture (design)

### 4a. Freeze seam + reuse map
Freeze at the **trajectory CSV → pure-Python replay**. One shared helper `backend/eval/golden_fixtures.py::replay_fixture()` is the *single* code path used by both the freeze tool and both gates:
`parse_trajectory_csv → windowing.build_windows(preset) → distill_local.build_candidates → rally_gate net_crossings → experiment.predict(CONTROL) → merge_overlaps → metrics.score`.
Reuses **verbatim**: `metrics.score/interval_iou`, `segmentation_metrics`, `experiment.predict/compare/Variant.spec_hash`, `windowing.WindowingPreset` (default SERVED), `regression.gt_hash/save_baseline + incommensurability guards`, `eval_stats` (seed=0), `eval_baselines/` committed-baseline precedent.

### 4b. Tiered model (SEALED-FIXTURES)
| Tier | Where | Key? | Contents | Test behaviour |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Tier-0** | `eval_baselines/fixtures/` (tracked) | none | opaque `fixture_id`, fps/frame_width (quantized), relative `start`/`end`, **5 shuttle scalars**, label, relative `gts`. **No person/audio/pose.** | **Unconditional** — runs on every PR/fork; turns today's skipped litmus into a real public gate |
| **Tier-1** | OneDrive (default) **or** SOPS-encrypted in-repo | token/age key | full corpus **with** person/audio; authoritative ΔF1/CI/sign-test + exact Gate-A litmus | **Skips cleanly** when key/data absent (generalize `_golden_present()` → `_tier1_present()`) |

A fresh no-key clone is always **green**; coverage never depends on a secret.

### 4c. Anti-attribution measures (Tier-0)
- **Opaque RANDOM surrogate id** (`os.urandom` + a private surrogate→source map kept off-git) — *not* the MD5 `video_id`, and *not* HMAC(salt, video_id‖name) (red-team: brute-forceable over a tiny known roster if the salt leaks). Strip the `name` field (today = source filename).
- **Metadata strip** — no filename, video_id, match/event/player identity, capture date.
- **Time-origin reset** — subtract per-fixture `t0` from all start/end/gts. **Provably metric-invariant** (`metrics.interval_iou` and start/end-error are pure differences) → bit-identical scores, severed absolute timeline.
- **Stream minimization** — Tier-0 keeps shuttle block only; person/audio excluded; pose/gait/face have **no field in the schema by design**.
- **Raw-trajectory exclusion** — never commit the per-frame `(Frame,Visibility,X,Y)` CSV.
- **Quantize fps/frame_width** — red-team: at n=6 the raw values are a camera/venue quasi-identifier.
- **Provenance hard-fail gate** — the de-attribution pass refuses to emit a public fixture for any record not tagged owner-recorded(Fork-A) + minors-excluded + biometric-excluded.

### 4d. The two gates
- **Characterization/snapshot** — replay fixture, **parse-compare** JSON (never byte-compare → CRLF/float-format safe; `core.autocrlf=true` here), exact on int/structural fields, `approx(abs=1e-6)` on floats; stamp + check windowing-preset/label/gt fingerprints (incommensurable → loud fail). Catches any behaviour change even at constant F1. **CONTROL path only** across machines.
- **Quality-floor** — score vs committed labels; assert per-video + mean F1 ≥ floor in `eval_baselines/regression_baseline.json` (created here; carries a **windowing fingerprint** so a served-default change is flagged incommensurable, not silently compared); emit `eval_stats` paired-delta-CI + sign test (NUMBERS INFORM); add a **metrics-vs-base-branch** paired delta. Synthetic-tier floors measure **correctness, not field quality** (documented).

### 4e. Serialization & optional protobuf
JSON with `sort_keys=True` + pre-quantized floats + LF is sufficient. **Protobuf is optional (O1)** — adopt only if cross-OS byte-stable snapshots are wanted (proto3 `rally.fixture.v1`, codegen + `protoc && git diff --exit-code` guard, `*.pb binary -text`). Protobuf is **encoding, not secrecy** (trivially `--decode_raw`).

### 4f. Encryption & keys (Tier-1 only)
**Default = out-of-repo + token** (existing OneDrive `RALLY_GOLDEN_DIR` seam — zero sensitive bytes in git history, sidesteps the legal "redistribution" act). **Alternative = SOPS + age** (per-recipient, revocable, value-level diffs) for Fork-A-clean-but-private data wanting in-repo versioning. Rejected: git-crypt (whole-repo re-key), git-lfs (storage ≠ secrecy). CI secret exposed **only to the Tier-1 job on trusted branches, never to fork-PR runners**. The **HMAC/surrogate salt** (if used) and the surrogate→source map live with the private corpus, rotated per release. Document explicitly that Tier-1 crypto defends **only** the no-key public.

---

## 5. Threat model (who's protected vs not)

| Actor | Protected against | NOT protected (by design / residual) |
|---|---|---|
| **TM1 no-key public** | map fixture→source/match/player; decrypt Tier-1; confirm a source even holding the candidate file | read Tier-0 numbers + run shuttle-only harness (intended; useless-in-isolation) |
| **TM2 keyed contributor** | recover filename/video_id/match/face/pose/gait — *never written* (survives the analog hole) | read every value the suite reads (unpreventable; not pretended otherwise) |
| **TM3 CI runner** | leaked log can't leak attribution it never had | runs the suite (intended); Tier-1 secret never on fork runners |
| **TM4 malicious keyed insider** | correlation needs a public reference; Fork-A source is unpublished → "not reasonably likely" | **residual:** conditional + revocable — publish/leak the source later and the link can snap back (forward-looking re-test duty) |
| **TM5 re-ID adversary** | source unpublished + only ~5 invariant scalars/window + reset timeline → no back-match | becomes live if a Fork-A source is ever published |
| **TM6 accidental plaintext commit** | required CI leak-scan rejects MD5/known-name/non-surrogate/plaintext-SOPS | if the required check is disabled, a leak lands |

---

## 6. Honest limits — what a green suite does NOT prove

- Green proves only that the **deterministic post-trajectory transforms** are unchanged for the **frozen cases**. It does **not** cover: the WASB/TrackNet detector, `_probe_fps_width`/cv2 frame-seek, the AI/provider handoff, chunking/re-encode, the ffmpeg stitch, DB persistence/migrations, or the API filter. *A refactor that breaks decoding or the detector passes these gates green.* The owner-box `regression.py` DB run remains the only full-chain check.
- Characterization = **behaviour-locking, not correctness** — it freezes current behaviour *including current bugs*. "Passing" = "behaviour unchanged", never "behaviour correct". This caveat must ride in the **test pass/fail message**, not only the docs.
- **Synthetic-tier floors = plumbing correctness, NOT field quality.** Never cite synthetic F1 in `QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md` / the paper as rally-detection performance (tag `kind: synthetic`).
- Tier-0 public coverage is **shuttle-only**; the person/audio fusion ΔF1 and exact Gate-A values need Tier-1 (skipped on no-key clones).
- Data is **not "anonymous"** absolutely (EDPS v. SRB) — relative, conditional, revocable, forward-looking.
- Encryption gives **no secrecy from a running contributor**; protobuf gives **no secrecy at all**.
- De-attribution does **not** cure provenance/licensing — it sits *behind* the per-record provenance gate + counsel sign-off.
- `--refresh-snapshot` can rubber-stamp a real regression; mitigation is **review of the visible diff** + a separate, deliberate floor-baseline update. Require a recorded reason.

---

## 8. Open owner / counsel questions

**Counsel (gating — block P2/P3):**
1. Does the EU/UK **sui-generis database right** subsist in our self-derived shuttle/rally streams on the *Sportradar* "obtained vs created" reasoning, and does redistributing a de-attributed Tier-0 subset cause *CV-Online* harm?
2. Post *Thomson Reuters v. Ross* + USCO-2025, is **US fair use for the extraction step** defensible if the dataset could compete in a "data/analytics" market — does publishing only numbers (never footage) matter?
3. India: are anti-scraping ToS enforceable; **IT Act s.43/s.66** exposure for any 3rd-party ingestion; does pending *ANI v. OpenAI* (~Apr 2026) move it? Confirm **DPDP s.9/Rule 10** and whether per-player tracking is prohibited s.9(3) monitoring of a minor.
4. Sign-off on the **final committed Tier-0 subset + provenance log**; is non-attributability-via-private-source a defensible posture to assert given its conditional/revocable nature?

**Owner (strategic):**
5. Publishing the Fork-A subset **forfeits the trade-secret moat** (Indian breach-of-confidence framing) — weighed against community/benchmark/paper aims. Your call.
6. **Forward-looking commitment:** do not publish a highlight reel of the *same* source whose fixtures are committed (without re-clearing), and accept a periodic re-assessment as the public-video environment grows.
7. **Scope of first public commit:** synthetic-only (zero provenance risk, recommended) vs a counsel-cleared real subset.
8. **Tier-1 mechanism:** out-of-repo+token (recommended) vs SOPS+age in-repo. And: is protobuf (O1) wanted, or is canonical-JSON enough?

## 9. Definition of done

The project is **DONE** (flip `Status → DONE`, archive) when:
- [ ] **P0, M1–M4, P1, DOC** are ☑ and merged.
- [ ] A clean `git clone` on **win + ubuntu + mac** runs `python run_all_tests.py` and the **Tier-0 gates pass green with no key / no video / no GPU / no DB**.
- [ ] The **CI leak-scan is a required status check** and passes (no MD5 / real-name / non-surrogate / plaintext-SOPS in the tree).

The spine: **no special-casing in the decision path** (signals→scorer, never `if/else`). Worked examples: a service-fault analyzer, a warm-up/practice span detector, and consuming a per-frame shuttle-state detector. A peer to `EXPERIMENT_HARNESS.md` and `MULTI_SIGNAL_FUSION_PLAN.md` (ADR-007 modular rally layer).

Substantial design/project work (anything bigger than a single small PR — multi-PR efforts, research-backed designs, security/privacy/architecture plans) is captured as a **tracked project doc** in `docs/`, following `docs/PROJECT_DOC_TEMPLATE.md`.

Shape: status header (`DRAFT → IN PROGRESS → DONE → archived`) · decisions-locked table · (optional) research/legal **foundation** · design + **reuse map** · (optional) **threat model** · **honest-limits** · **§7 progress tracker** (one ☐/◐/☑ row per independently-shippable PR — the source of truth) · open owner/counsel questions · **definition-of-done** (acceptance checklist) · references · changelog. Tag each claim `[verified]` / `[researched]` / `[design]`.

**Why:** the owner explicitly loved this framing (2026-06-16, the Golden Regression Fixtures plan) and asked to make it a norm "throughout" — it lets a plan be iterated on, its progress tracked across sessions, and be marked COMPLETE against clear deliverables (then archived). Generalizes the emergent pattern already in `docs/RALLY_QUALITY_RESEARCH.md` §7.

**How to apply:** for a substantial ask, write/extend the doc FIRST (draft, owner-gated), index it in `docs/README.md` + a memory `current-state` pointer (+ `docs/NEXT_STEPS.md` once work starts); each deliverable = ONE small PR per [[gotcha-shared-checkout-branch-collisions]]; flip Status and archive when the definition-of-done is met. ## 1. EXACT FUNCTION SIGNATURES + RETURN TYPES

### **tracknet_runner.py**

```python
parse_trajectory_csv(csv_win_path: str) -> List[TrajectoryPoint]
  # Returns sorted list of TrajectoryPoint(frame: int, visible: bool, x: float, y: float)
  # Parses CSV with columns: Frame, Visibility (0|1), X, Y
  # Silently skips malformed rows (KeyError/ValueError)

trajectory_to_action_windows(
    points: List[TrajectoryPoint],
    fps: float,
    frame_width: float,
    chunk_start: float = 0.0,
    velocity_thresh: float = 0.02,
    merge_gap: float = 2.0,
    min_window_duration: float = 2.0,
    chunk_index: Optional[int] = None,
    max_window_duration: float = 0.0,
    min_split_gap: float = 0.5,
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]
  # Returns: [{
  #   "start": float (seconds),
  #   "end": float (seconds), 
  #   "active_frame_count": int,
  #   "peak_velocity": float,
  #   "mean_velocity": float,
  #   "track_id_count": int (always 1),
  #   "chunk_index": Optional[int]
  # }]
  # OCCLUSION: visible==False breaks trajectory (sets prev=None, resets velocity chain)
  # CLAMPS: fps<=0 → 30.0; frame_width<=0 → 1280.0
  # BOUNDED WINDOWING (#187): max_window_duration > 0 triggers _split_overlong_groups()
  #   → recursively splits windows > max_window_duration at largest inactivity gap
  #   (gap >= min_split_gap frames), never merges, only subdivides
```

### **windowing.py**

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WindowingPreset:
    name: str
    velocity_thresh: float
    merge_gap: float
    min_window_duration: float
    max_window_duration: float = 0.0
    
    def as_tuple(self) -> Tuple[float, float, float]
        # Returns (velocity_thresh, merge_gap, min_window_duration)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]

SERVED: WindowingPreset   # sourced from IndexingConfig defaults
  # name="served", velocity_thresh=0.02, merge_gap=2.0, 
  # min_window_duration=2.0, max_window_duration=0.0 (default OFF)

PROPOSAL: WindowingPreset
  # name="proposal", velocity_thresh=0.005, merge_gap=1.0,
  # min_window_duration=0.5, max_window_duration=0.0

PRESETS: Dict[str, WindowingPreset]  # {"served": SERVED, "proposal": PROPOSAL}

served_windowing_from_config(config: Any, family: str = "wasb") -> WindowingPreset
  # Reads actual served config (overrides honoured)

build_windows(
    points: List[Any],
    fps: float,
    frame_width: float,
    preset: WindowingPreset
) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]
  # Thin wrapper: calls TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows with preset params

windows_to_intervals(windows: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Interval]
  # Returns [(w["start"], w["end"]) for w in windows]
  # Interval = Tuple[float, float]
```

### **distill_local.py**

```python
FEATURE_NAMES: List[str] = ["duration", "peak_velocity", "mean_velocity", "active_ratio", "net_crossings"]
PROPOSAL = (0.005, 1.0, 0.5)  # velocity_thresh, merge_gap, min_window_duration
BASELINE_LOCAL = (0.02, 2.0, 2.0)  # the SERVED windowing

build_candidates(
    trajectory_csv: str,
    fps: float,
    frame_width: float,
    proposal: Tuple[float, float, float] = PROPOSAL
) -> Tuple[List[Interval], List[List[float]]]
  # Returns (candidate_intervals, feature_rows)
  # Interval = Tuple[float, float]
  # feature_rows: each row = [duration, peak_velocity, mean_velocity, active_ratio, net_crossings]
  # Net crossings sourced from rally_gate.gate_windows(..., min_crossings=0) → .crossings field
```

### **experiment.py**

```python
@dataclass
class VideoCandidates:
    name: str
    intervals: List[Interval]
    features: Dict[str, List[float]] = field(default_factory=dict)
      # Column-major: feature_name → list of per-candidate values
    gts: Optional[List[Interval]] = None
    # Interval = Tuple[float, float]

@dataclass
class Variant:
    name: str
    segmenter: str = "wasb_hybrid"
    config_overrides: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    cue_flags: Dict[str, bool] = field(default_factory=dict)
    min_crossings: int = 0  # Gate A (net-crossing threshold)
    min_players: int = 0    # Gate B (player count threshold)
    classifier_model: Optional[str] = None  # path to frozen LogisticRegression JSON
    feature_names: Optional[List[str]] = None  # which persisted features to use
    threshold: float = 0.5  # classifier probability cutoff
    merge_gap: float = 1.0  # post-filter overlap-merge gap (seconds)
    tune_threshold: bool = False  # pick F1-best threshold on train fold
    calibrate: Optional[str] = None  # "platt" | "isotonic" | None
    
    def is_learned(self) -> bool
        # True iff (classifier_model is not None) OR (feature_names is not None)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> dict
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "Variant"
    
    def spec_hash(self) -> str
        # Stable 12-char SHA1 hash of to_dict() (json sorted)

CONTROL = Variant(name="control")
  # Baseline: all fields at defaults (no gate, no learned filter, pure merge)
  # is_learned() → False
  # predict(CONTROL, ...) uses ONLY _gate_mask (trivial, all True) + merge_overlaps
  # PURE STDLIB (no numpy/LogisticRegression)

predict(
    variant: Variant,
    vc: VideoCandidates,
    model: Optional[LogisticRegression] = None,
    threshold: Optional[float] = None,
    calibrator: Optional[Callable[[float], float]] = None,
) -> List[Interval]
  # 1. _gate_mask(variant, vc) → boolean keep-mask
  #    - if min_crossings > 0: read "net_crossings" column, apply threshold
  #    - if min_players > 0: read "players_in_court" column, apply threshold
  #    - missing columns default to [0.0] * n_candidates (logs warning)
  # 2. if variant.feature_names:
  #    - load model (supplied, or from variant.classifier_model)
  #    - predict_proba(feature_matrix) → probs
  #    - optionally calibrate(proba)
  #    - apply threshold → additional mask
  # 3. kept = [iv for iv, keep in zip(vc.intervals, mask) if keep]
  # 4. return merge_overlaps(kept, gap_tol=variant.merge_gap)
```

### **metrics.py**

```python
Interval = Tuple[float, float]

@dataclass
class Score:
    iou_threshold: float
    num_pred: int
    num_gt: int
    true_positives: int
    false_positives: int
    false_negatives: int
    precision: float
    recall: float
    f1: float
    mean_iou: float
    mean_start_error: float
    mean_end_error: float
    
    def as_dict(self) -> dict

interval_iou(a: Interval, b: Interval) -> float
  # Temporal IoU; returns 0.0 if non-overlapping or degenerate

match_intervals(
    preds: List[Interval],
    gts: List[Interval],
    iou_threshold: float = 0.5
) -> MatchResult
  # Greedy one-to-one matching by descending IoU
  # Returns MatchResult(matches, unmatched_pred_indices, unmatched_gt_indices)

score(
    preds: List[Interval],
    gts: List[Interval],
    iou_threshold: float = 0.5,
    match_result: Optional[MatchResult] = None
) -> Score
  # Computes precision, recall, F1, mean_iou, boundary errors
```

### **gt_loader.py**

```python
load_gt_intervals(csv_path: str, *, strict: bool = True) -> List[Interval]
  # strict=True: raise ValueError on bad rows (corpus/gate use)
  # strict=False: skip bad rows, LOG count (legacy tuning-driver behavior)
  # Accepts both header conventions: "start"/"end" OR "start_time"/"end_time"
  # Positional fallback if no header: columns 0,1
  # Timestamps: decimal seconds or MM:SS / HH:MM:SS
  # Returns sorted by start; warns on overlapping GTs
```

### **rally_gate.py**

```python
@dataclass
class GatedWindow:
    start: float
    end: float
    crossings: int       # net crossing count
    visible_points: int
    kept: bool           # crossings >= min_crossings

estimate_play_axis(points) -> Tuple[str, float, float]
  # Returns (axis: "x"|"y", net_value: float, span: float)
  # Axis = the image dimension with larger shuttle spread (play axis)
  # net_value = median of that axis (where shuttle clusters near net)
  # span = spread (p95 - p5) for that axis

count_net_crossings(window_points, axis: str, net: float, deadband: float) -> int
  # Counts sign changes of (coord - net) across visible points
  # Ignores points within deadband of the net

gate_windows(
    points,
    windows: List[Interval],
    fps: float,
    min_crossings: int = 2,
    deadband_frac: float = 0.06,
    estimate: Optional[Tuple[str, float, float]] = None
) -> List[GatedWindow]
  # Annotates each window with crossing count and keep decision
  # estimate parameter: precomputed (axis, net, span) to avoid O(P log P) re-estimation
  # deadband = deadband_frac * span
  # Returns sorted by window start time

apply_gate(
    points,
    windows: List[Interval],
    fps: float,
    min_crossings: int = 2,
    deadband_frac: float = 0.06
) -> List[Interval]
  # Convenience: returns only windows where kept=True
```

### **fusion_compare.py** (loader pattern)

```python
load_videos(features_dir: str) -> List[experiment.VideoCandidates]
  # Loads every *_golden_features.json in features_dir
  # For each: reads c["shuttle"][fn] and c["person"][fn] UNCONDITIONALLY
  #   (no .get() fallback — KeyError if missing)
  # Returns VideoCandidates with features={shuttle_cols + person_cols}
```

### **ablation.py** (loader pattern)

```python
load_videos(features_dir: str) -> List[VideoCandidates]
  # Loads every *_golden_features.json
  # For shuttle + person: reads unconditionally c["shuttle"][fn], c["person"][fn]
  # For audio: reads c.get("audio") or {} → .get(fn, 0.0) with fallback to 0.0
  # Older JSONs without audio block: all audio columns default to [0.0]
```

## 2. CONCRETE INTEGRATION RECIPE (CONTROL-only, pure stdlib)

```python
# Step 1: Load and parse trajectory CSV
points: List[TrajectoryPoint] = TrackNetRunner.parse_trajectory_csv(trajectory_csv)

# Step 2: Window the trajectory (use SERVED preset from windowing.py)
from backend.eval.windowing import SERVED, build_windows
windows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = build_windows(points, fps=30.0, frame_width=1280.0, preset=SERVED)

# Step 3: Build candidates (extract features including net_crossings via rally_gate)
from backend.eval.distill_local import build_candidates
intervals, X = build_candidates(trajectory_csv, fps=30.0, frame_width=1280.0)
# intervals: List[Interval]
# X: List[List[float]] with 5 columns [duration, peak_velocity, mean_velocity, active_ratio, net_crossings]

# Step 4: Gate on net-crossings (rally_gate.gate_windows is called within build_candidates)
from backend.pipeline.detectors import rally_gate
gated = rally_gate.gate_windows(points, intervals, fps=30.0, min_crossings=2)
# gated: List[GatedWindow]

# Step 5: Score predictions against golden labels
from backend.eval.gt_loader import load_gt_intervals
gts: List[Interval] = load_gt_intervals(gt_csv_path, strict=True)

# Step 6: Create VideoCandidates structure for experiment harness
from backend.eval.experiment import VideoCandidates, CONTROL, predict
vc = VideoCandidates(
    name="test_video",
    intervals=intervals,
    features={},  # CONTROL doesn't use features
    gts=gts
)

# Step 7: Predict with CONTROL variant
preds: List[Interval] = predict(CONTROL, vc)
# predict() for CONTROL:
#   - mask = [True] * len(intervals)  (no gates, feature_names=None)
#   - kept = intervals  (all pass)
#   - return merge_overlaps(intervals, gap_tol=1.0)

# Step 8: Score
from backend.eval.metrics import score as score_metrics
result = score_metrics(preds, gts, iou_threshold=0.5)
# result.precision, result.recall, result.f1, result.mean_iou, 
# result.true_positives, result.false_positives, result.false_negatives
```

**EXACT CALL CHAIN (no branches):**
```
parse_trajectory_csv(csv) 
  → List[TrajectoryPoint]
  
build_windows(points, fps, fw, SERVED) 
  → calls TrackNetRunner.trajectory_to_action_windows(vt=0.02, mg=2.0, mwd=2.0, max_win=0)
  → List[Dict]
  
windows_to_intervals(windows) 
  → List[Interval]
  
gate_windows(points, intervals, fps, min_crossings=2)
  → List[GatedWindow] (crossings field used by build_candidates)
  
predict(CONTROL, VideoCandidates)
  → _gate_mask(CONTROL, vc)  # all True
  → merge_overlaps(intervals, gap_tol=1.0)
  → List[Interval]

score(preds, gts, 0.5)
  → Score object
```

## 3. RED-TEAM GOTCHAS (CONFIRMED AGAINST CODE)

**(a) Do existing loaders read GT via LENIENT vs STRICT?**

**YES — INCONSISTENT (NOT FIXED)** [gotcha confirmed]
- `calibrate_wasb.load_golden_gts()` → calls `load_gt_intervals(csv_path, strict=False)` 
  - **File: backend/eval/calibrate_wasb.py:41**
  - Silently skips bad rows, logs count
- `experiment.evaluate_variant()` and `distill_local.load_video()` call `load_golden_gts(spec["labels"])` (from calibrate_wasb)
  - Uses lenient loader, not strict
- The strict loader `load_gt_intervals(csv_path, strict=True)` exists but is **NOT used by the eval loaders**
- **Impact:** A GT CSV with malformed rows (missing columns, bad timestamps) silently shrinks the GT set, flattering precision

**(b) Does experiment `_feature_column` silently default MISSING features to 0.0?**

**YES** [gotcha confirmed]
- **File: backend/eval/experiment.py:180-192**
  ```python
  def _feature_column(vc: VideoCandidates, name: str) -> List[float]:
      col = vc.features.get(name)
      n = len(vc.intervals)
      if col is None:
          logger.warning("variant references feature %r absent from %s — treating as 0.0",
                         name, vc.name)
          return [0.0] * n  # SILENT ZERO FILL
      # ...rest
  ```
- Logs a warning but DOES NOT raise; missing feature columns silently become 0.0
- This lets a sparse/old fixture still predict (gates + learned scorer work with zeros)

**(c) Does any loader read c['person'] UNCONDITIONALLY (KeyError on stripped fixture)?**

**YES — CRITICAL** [gotcha confirmed]
- **fusion_compare.py:48** `features[fn] = [float(c["person"][fn]) for c in cands]`
  - Direct subscript access, no `.get()` fallback
  - If a fixture has NO "person" key in any candidate, **KeyError** on the first loop
  - **File: backend/eval/fusion_compare.py:35-52**
- **ablation.py:401** Same pattern: `features[fn] = [float(c["person"][fn]) for c in cands]`
  - **File: backend/eval/ablation.py:386-407**
- **ablation.py:403** Audio HAS defensive `.get()`: `c.get("audio") or {}.get(fn, 0.0)`
  - Audio defaults to 0.0 if missing; person does NOT
- **Impact:** A fixture with person-cue stripped (e.g., for privacy) will crash both fusion_compare and ablation at load time

**(d) Does CONTROL avoid numpy/LogisticRegression entirely (pure stdlib)? Which variants are is_learned()?**

**YES — CONTROL is pure stdlib; learned variants use numpy/LogisticRegression** [gotcha confirmed]
- **CONTROL definition (backend/eval/experiment.py:158)**:
  ```python
  CONTROL = Variant(name="control")  # all fields at defaults
  ```
  - `feature_names=None`, `classifier_model=None`, `min_crossings=0`, `min_players=0`
  - `is_learned()` → `False`
  - `predict(CONTROL, vc)` → calls `_gate_mask` (stdlib only) + `merge_overlaps` (stdlib only)
  - **NO numpy, NO LogisticRegression**
  
- **is_learned() definition (backend/eval/experiment.py:123-125)**:
  ```python
  def is_learned(self) -> bool:
      return self.classifier_model is not None or bool(self.feature_names)
  ```
  - Learned: any variant with `classifier_model` set OR `feature_names` set
  - Learned variants call `LogisticRegression.load()` (requires numpy)
  - **File: backend/eval/experiment.py:233** `model = LogisticRegression.load(variant.classifier_model)`
  - **File: backend/eval/experiment.py:234** `proba = [float(p) for p in model.predict_proba(...)]`

- **Examples of is_learned() variants**:
  - `fusion_compare.shuttle_only`: `feature_names=list(SHUTTLE_FEATURE_NAMES)`
  - `ablation.learned_full_variant()`: `feature_names=[all 13 columns]`
  - Any variant with `classifier_model="path/to/model.json"` set

---

## SUMMARY

All exact APIs mapped. The CONTROL variant is production-ready stdlib code (pure Python, no ML deps). The integration recipe is deterministic and reproducible: trajectory CSV → windows → candidates → predict(CONTROL) → score(preds, gts). Three critical loader gotchas confirmed: (a) lenient GT reading flattens precision, (b) missing features silently become 0.0, (c) **person cue strips cause KeyError on fusion_compare/ablation** — this is the highest-risk one for your `replay_fixture()` if feeding a CSV with no person data. CONTROL avoids all ML entirely.

## Summary

Implemented deliverable **M1** (Fixture framework + synthetic Tier-0 + shared replay helper) of the Golden Regression Fixtures project, entirely within the `golden-fixtures` worktree. No git commands beyond read-only `status` for verification.

### Files created

- `eval_baselines/fixtures/&lt;slug&gt;/{trajectory.csv, labels.csv, expected.json, provenance.json}` for the 3 slugs (generated by `--freeze-synthetic`, never hand-written).
- `eval_baselines/fixtures/manifest.json` — array of `{slug, fps, frame_width, windowing:{vt,mg,mwd,max_win}, kind:"synthetic", schema_version, paths}`.
- `eval_baselines/fixtures/README.md` — guardrails + freeze/refresh commands + "generated, never hand-edited" + §6 honest-limits.
- `.gitattributes` (worktree root) — `eval_baselines/fixtures/** text eol=lf`.
- `tests/test_golden_fixtures.py` — parametrized over `load_manifest()` slugs.

### Verify results (verbatim)
1. `--freeze-synthetic` → `froze 3 synthetic fixture(s): clean_single_rally, multi_rally_with_deadtime, occlusion_break`. `--check` → all `[ok]`, `EXIT=0`.
2. `python run_all_tests.py` → **744 passed, 7 skipped** (the pre-existing skips; new file adds 17 tests, all green). With CI coverage flags: **Total coverage 80.36%** ("Required test coverage of 70% reached"); the new module itself is at 85%.
3. `ruff check .` → `All checks passed!` (EXIT 0). `mypy backend training` → `Success: no issues found in 103 source files` (EXIT 0). One initial ruff finding (unused `field` import) fixed.

### The 3 synthetic scenarios (num candidates, num segments, F1)
| slug | candidates | segments | F1 | what it exercises |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `clean_single_rally` | 1 | 1 | 1.0 | one ~4s in-flight rally → single window, net_crossings=7 |
| `multi_rally_with_deadtime` | 2 | 2 | 1.0 | two rallies split by 3s of **still** (low-velocity) dead-time |
| `occlusion_break` | 2 | 2 | 1.0 | two rallies split by 3s of **occlusion** (Visibility=0) |

### Hard-constraint compliance
- **CONTROL/pure-stdlib only**: builds `VideoCandidates` directly with only the 5 shuttle columns and replays via `experiment.predict(CONTROL, vc)`; never calls `fusion_compare.load_videos`/`ablation.load_videos` (verified `CONTROL.is_learned() is False`).
- **Parse-compare, never byte-compare**: `compare_expected` does `json.load` on both sides — exact on int/structural fields (candidate count, `net_crossings` as int, TP/FP/FN, segment count, slug, schema, gt_hash) and abs-tol 1e-6 on floats.
- **Windowing pinned per fixture**: `(vt=0.02, mg=2.0, mwd=2.0, max_win=0.0)` written into each `provenance.json` and the manifest, read back and passed explicitly to `build_candidates` — does not depend on the named `SERVED` constant.
- **Determinism**: trajectories from deterministic sine formulae (no `random`); all floats rounded to 6dp; UTF-8 / LF-only / trailing-newline confirmed.
- **Privacy**: every fixture carries `kind:"synthetic"`, `minors_free:true`, `license:"synthetic"`; `expected.json` has no person/pose/gait/face/audio/player fields (positive assertion test scans the raw text and the parsed candidate keys).

Beyond the spec's required tests I added `test_check_cli_passes`, `test_synthetic_provenance_tags`, `test_control_is_not_learned`, and `test_manifest_is_non_empty`, and I additionally demonstrated out-of-band that `--check` returns exit 1 on a seeded trajectory perturbation (then restored) — proving the gate catches behaviour changes, not just echoes the snapshot.

### Deviations from spec
- **One** minor wording in `test_perturbing_trajectory_changes_segments`: I perturb by **truncating** the trajectory to its first half (which shrinks the derived window's `end`) rather than deleting an interior hole. Reason: deleting interior rows does not move the segment boundaries — `merge_gap=2.0s` coalesces across the gap and the surviving rows keep their original frame indices, so start/end are unchanged. Truncation guarantees a structural change and still proves the windows are re-derived from the CSV (not echoed). No API or fixture-content deviation otherwise.

Note: numpy and cv2 *are* installed in this environment, so the full suite ran here rather than skipping — but the M1 replay path itself touches no numpy (CONTROL/stdlib), so it remains valid on a clean no-GPU clone.</result>
