### 142. user (2026-06-16T04:53:40.580Z)

1	"""Golden Regression Fixtures (M1) — video-free, CONTROL-only characterization harness.
2	
3	`docs/GOLDEN_REGRESSION_FIXTURES.md` (§4, §6, §7). This module is the **single shared
4	replay code path** used by both the freeze tool and the characterization gate, and it
5	deliberately stays on the **pure-stdlib CONTROL path** (D5 in that doc): the learned
6	variants train a numpy/OpenBLAS logistic regression whose near-threshold decisions flip
7	across CPUs, so they can never anchor a cross-machine byte/parse snapshot. Here we replay
8	only `experiment.CONTROL` (`is_learned() == False`), which keeps all candidate intervals,
9	merges overlaps, and uses **no numpy**.
10	
11	The replay reuses the already-shipped eval stack VERBATIM::
12	
13	    parse_trajectory_csv → distill_local.build_candidates(proposal=pinned)
14	        → VideoCandidates(name, intervals, features={5 shuttle cols}, gts=load_gt_intervals)
15	        → experiment.predict(CONTROL, vc) → metrics.score(preds, gts, iou)
16	
17	Tier-0 / privacy invariants (D3, §4c, §6 of the doc), enforced here by construction:
18	
19	* Fixtures are **synthetic** — deterministic formulae, NO randomness — so they carry zero
20	  provenance/licensing risk and are safe to commit publicly (owner Q7 "synthetic-only").
21	* Every fixture is tagged ``kind="synthetic"``, ``minors_free=true``, ``license="synthetic"``.
22	* ``expected.json`` carries **only the 5 shuttle feature columns** — there is, by design, no
23	  person / pose / gait / face / audio field anywhere in the schema (positive-assertion test).
24	* The windowing preset params ``(vt, mg, mwd, max_win)`` are **pinned inside each fixture's
25	  provenance** and passed explicitly to ``build_candidates`` — we do NOT rely on the named
26	  ``windowing.SERVED`` constant, so a future SERVED retune cannot silently churn fixtures.
27	
28	§6 honest-limits caveat (carried in the CLI / test messages, not only the docs): a green
29	suite proves only that the deterministic post-trajectory transforms are unchanged for the
30	frozen synthetic cases — NOT that the detector/decoder/AI handoff/DB are correct, and NOT
31	that rally detection is good. Synthetic-tier numbers measure plumbing correctness, never
32	field quality (never cite them in QUALITY_ITERATIONS.md / the paper).
33	
34	Pure + offline + stdlib. The replay imports `tracknet_runner` (which is itself stdlib-only
35	for the parse/window math — no TF), but pulls in no numpy/cv2 on the CONTROL path.
36	"""
37	from __future__ import annotations
38	
39	import argparse
40	import json
41	import math
42	import sys
43	from dataclasses import dataclass
44	from pathlib import Path
45	from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple
46	
47	# --- shipped eval stack, reused verbatim (CONTROL / pure-stdlib path only) -------------
48	from backend.eval import distill_local
49	from backend.eval.experiment import CONTROL, VideoCandidates, predict
50	from backend.eval.gt_loader import load_gt_intervals
51	from backend.eval.metrics import score
52	from backend.eval.regression import gt_hash
53	
54	Interval = Tuple[float, float]
55	
56	#: Bump when the fixture/expected schema changes shape. Loaders refuse a fixture whose
57	#: ``schema_version`` exceeds this (forward-incompatible), per the schema-drift guard.
58	CURRENT_SCHEMA = 1
59	
60	#: Repo-root-relative fixtures dir, resolved from THIS file (never cwd) so the harness works
61	#: from any working directory / a fresh clone. ``parents[2]`` = backend/eval → backend → root.
62	FIXTURES_DIR: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "eval_baselines" / "fixtures"
63	
64	#: The pinned SERVED windowing the synthetic fixtures freeze at: (velocity_thresh, merge_gap,
65	#: min_window_duration). Mirrors the historical SERVED values (0.02, 2.0, 2.0) but is a LITERAL
66	#: here on purpose — the spec forbids depending on the named ``windowing.SERVED`` constant so a
67	#: future retune of SERVED can never silently re-baseline a committed fixture. Each fixture also
68	#: re-states these in its provenance; the replay reads them from there.
69	PINNED_VT = 0.02
70	PINNED_MERGE_GAP = 2.0
71	PINNED_MIN_WINDOW = 2.0
72	PINNED_MAX_WINDOW = 0.0  # 0 = bounded-windowing OFF (W1), matching the SERVED default
73	
74	#: The 5 fps/resolution-invariant shuttle feature columns produced by distill_local. Re-stated
75	#: here (not imported as a mutable alias) so a fixture's expected.json is pinned to exactly these.
76	SHUTTLE_FEATURES: Tuple[str, ...] = (
77	    "duration", "peak_velocity", "mean_velocity", "active_ratio", "net_crossings",
78	)
79	assert tuple(distill_local.FEATURE_NAMES) == SHUTTLE_FEATURES, (
80	    "distill_local.FEATURE_NAMES drifted from the pinned shuttle feature set"
81	)
82	
83	#: Forbidden key substrings — biometric / identity / non-shuttle streams that MUST NOT appear
84	#: anywhere in a Tier-0 fixture (D3 / §4c). The privacy assertion scans for these.
85	FORBIDDEN_KEYS: Tuple[str, ...] = ("person", "pose", "gait", "face", "audio", "player")
86	
87	_FLOAT_ROUND_DP = 6  # all written floats rounded to this many decimals (determinism)
88	_FLOAT_TOL = 1e-6    # abs tolerance when parse-comparing recomputed vs committed floats
89	
90	
91	# ======================================================================================
92	# Synthetic trajectory generation (deterministic — NO random)
93	# ======================================================================================
94	
95	
96	@dataclass(frozen=True)
97	class RallySpec:
98	    """One in-flight rally segment: the shuttle oscillates across the net midline.
99	
100	    ``start_frame`` — first frame index; ``n_frames`` — length; ``period`` — oscillation
101	    period in frames (governs net-crossing count); ``amplitude`` — px excursion from the
102	    net midline along the play axis. Deterministic sine motion → fixed velocity/crossings.
103	    """
104	
105	    start_frame: int
106	    n_frames: int
107	    period: float = 30.0
108	    amplitude: float = 400.0
109	
110	
111	@dataclass(frozen=True)
112	class GapSpec:
113	    """Dead-time between rallies — what separates one window from the next.
114	
115	    ``kind="still"`` — shuttle visible but resting (velocity ≈ 0 → no active frames);
116	    ``kind="occluded"`` — shuttle not visible (Visibility=0 → trajectory broken). Both
117	    end an active run so the next rally forms a distinct window.
118	    """
119	
120	    start_frame: int
121	    n_frames: int
122	    kind: str = "still"  # "still" | "occluded"
123	
124	
125	@dataclass(frozen=True)
126	class FixtureSpec:
127	    """A full synthetic scenario: a frame-indexed sequence of rallies and gaps + its GT.
128	
129	    ``gts`` are the hand-authored ground-truth rally intervals (seconds) — the synthetic
130	    "labels.csv". For a synthetic fixture they line up with the rallies by construction,
131	    which is exactly what makes the quality-floor a *plumbing-correctness* check, not a
132	    field-quality one (§6).
133	    """
134	
135	    slug: str
136	    description: str
137	    fps: float
138	    frame_width: float
139	    frame_height: float
140	    segments: Tuple[Any, ...]  # ordered (RallySpec | GapSpec)
141	    gts: Tuple[Interval, ...]
142	    net_midline: float = 640.0
143	
144	
145	def _rally_points(spec: RallySpec, net_midline: float, frame_height: float
146	                  ) -> List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]]:
147	    """Deterministic in-flight shuttle: sine oscillation across the net midline (play axis = x)."""
148	    rows: List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]] = []
149	    y_mid = frame_height / 2.0
150	    for i in range(spec.n_frames):
151	        x = net_midline + spec.amplitude * math.sin(2.0 * math.pi * i / spec.period)
152	        # A small orthogonal wobble keeps y-spread < x-spread so the play axis is unambiguously x.
153	        y = y_mid + 30.0 * math.sin(2.0 * math.pi * i / (spec.period / 2.0))
154	        rows.append((spec.start_frame + i, 1, x, y))
155	    return rows
156	
157	
158	def _gap_points(spec: GapSpec, net_midline: float, frame_height: float
159	                ) -> List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]]:
160	    """Dead-time rows: resting-visible (still) or absent (occluded)."""
161	    rows: List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]] = []
162	    y_mid = frame_height / 2.0
163	    for i in range(spec.n_frames):
164	        if spec.kind == "occluded":
165	            rows.append((spec.start_frame + i, 0, 0.0, 0.0))
166	        elif spec.kind == "still":
167	            rows.append((spec.start_frame + i, 1, net_midline, y_mid))
168	        else:  # pragma: no cover - guarded by builder
169	            raise ValueError(f"unknown gap kind {spec.kind!r} (use 'still'|'occluded')")
170	    return rows
171	
172	
173	def make_synthetic_trajectory(spec: FixtureSpec) -> List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]]:
174	    """Build the deterministic ``(Frame, Visibility, X, Y)`` rows for a synthetic scenario."""
175	    rows: List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]] = []
176	    for seg in spec.segments:
177	        if isinstance(seg, RallySpec):
178	            rows.extend(_rally_points(seg, spec.net_midline, spec.frame_height))
179	        elif isinstance(seg, GapSpec):
180	            rows.extend(_gap_points(seg, spec.net_midline, spec.frame_height))
181	        else:  # pragma: no cover - guarded by dataclass shape
182	            raise TypeError(f"unknown segment type {type(seg).__name__}")
183	    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r[0])
184	    return rows
185	
186	
187	def write_trajectory_csv(rows: Sequence[Tuple[int, int, float, float]], path: Path) -> None:
188	    """Write trajectory rows as the TrackNet CSV (``Frame,Visibility,X,Y``), LF + 6dp floats."""
189	    lines = ["Frame,Visibility,X,Y"]
190	    for frame, vis, x, y in rows:
191	        lines.append(f"{int(frame)},{int(vis)},{_fmt(x)},{_fmt(y)}")
192	    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
193	
194	
195	def write_labels_csv(gts: Sequence[Interval], path: Path) -> None:
196	    """Write GT intervals as a ``start,end`` labels CSV (the strict gt_loader format), LF."""
197	    lines = ["start,end"]
198	    for s, e in gts:
199	        lines.append(f"{_fmt(s)},{_fmt(e)}")
200	    path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
201	
202	
203	# ======================================================================================
204	# Built-in synthetic scenarios (the 3 committed slugs)
205	# ======================================================================================
206	
207	
208	def _fr(seconds: float, fps: float) -> int:
209	    return int(round(seconds * fps))
210	
211	
212	def builtin_specs() -> List[FixtureSpec]:
213	    """The committed synthetic scenarios. Deterministic; chosen so each exercises a distinct
214	    windowing behaviour (one window / dead-time split / occlusion split)."""
215	    fps = 30.0
216	    fw, fh = 1280.0, 720.0
217	    net = 640.0
218	
219	    # 1) clean_single_rally: one ~4s in-flight rally → exactly 1 candidate window, crossings>0.
220	    single = FixtureSpec(
221	        slug="clean_single_rally",
222	        description="One continuous ~4s rally; shuttle oscillates across the net → 1 window.",
223	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, frame_height=fh, net_midline=net,
224	        segments=(RallySpec(start_frame=0, n_frames=120),),
225	        # Window is [0.033, 3.967]; GT is the rally span the synthetic motion fills.
226	        gts=((0.0, 4.0),),
227	    )
228	
229	    # 2) multi_rally_with_deadtime: rally, 3s STILL dead-time, rally → 2 windows split by the
230	    #    low-velocity gap (> merge_gap=2.0s) — proves dead-time separates windows.
231	    r0 = RallySpec(start_frame=0, n_frames=120)
232	    gap_still = GapSpec(start_frame=120, n_frames=_fr(3.0, fps), kind="still")
233	    r1 = RallySpec(start_frame=120 + _fr(3.0, fps), n_frames=120)
234	    multi = FixtureSpec(
235	        slug="multi_rally_with_deadtime",
236	        description="Two ~4s rallies separated by 3s of still (resting-shuttle) dead-time → 2 windows.",
237	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, frame_height=fh, net_midline=net,
238	        segments=(r0, gap_still, r1),
239	        gts=((0.0, 4.0), (7.0, 11.0)),
240	    )
241	
242	    # 3) occlusion_break: rally, 3s OCCLUDED (Visibility=0), rally → 2 windows split by the
243	    #    visibility break — proves occlusion (not just low velocity) separates windows.
244	    o0 = RallySpec(start_frame=0, n_frames=120)
245	    gap_occ = GapSpec(start_frame=120, n_frames=_fr(3.0, fps), kind="occluded")
246	    o1 = RallySpec(start_frame=120 + _fr(3.0, fps), n_frames=120)
247	    occ = FixtureSpec(
248	        slug="occlusion_break",
249	        description="Two ~4s rallies separated by 3s of shuttle occlusion (Visibility=0) → 2 windows.",
250	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, frame_height=fh, net_midline=net,
251	        segments=(o0, gap_occ, o1),
252	        gts=((0.0, 4.0), (7.0, 11.0)),
253	    )
254	
255	    return [single, multi, occ]
256	
257	
258	def _spec_by_slug() -> Dict[str, FixtureSpec]:
259	    return {s.slug: s for s in builtin_specs()}
260	
261	
262	# ======================================================================================
263	# The shared replay (CONTROL / pure-stdlib path)
264	# ======================================================================================
265	
266	
267	def _fmt(x: float) -> str:
268	    """Render a float with up to 6dp, no scientific notation, ``-0.0`` normalised to ``0.0``."""
269	    r = round(float(x) + 0.0, _FLOAT_ROUND_DP)
270	    if r == 0.0:
271	        r = 0.0
272	    return f"{r:.6f}"
273	
274	
275	def _round(x: float) -> float:
276	    r = round(float(x) + 0.0, _FLOAT_ROUND_DP)
277	    return 0.0 if r == 0.0 else r
278	
279	
280	def _pinned_proposal(prov: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
281	    """The windowing proposal tuple, read from the fixture provenance when present (the pinned
282	    params travel with the fixture), else the module-level pinned defaults. NEVER windowing.SERVED."""
283	    if prov and "windowing" in prov:
284	        w = prov["windowing"]
285	        return (float(w["vt"]), float(w["mg"]), float(w["mwd"]))
286	    return (PINNED_VT, PINNED_MERGE_GAP, PINNED_MIN_WINDOW)
287	
288	
289	def replay_fixture(slug_or_dir: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
290	    """Replay one fixture through the shipped CONTROL path; return its recomputed snapshot.
291	
292	    Resolves ``slug_or_dir`` (a slug string or a fixture directory ``Path``) to its committed
293	    ``trajectory.csv`` + ``labels.csv`` + ``provenance.json``, then:
294	
295	        parse_trajectory_csv → build_candidates(proposal=pinned) → VideoCandidates(5 shuttle
296	        cols only) → predict(CONTROL, vc) → score(preds, gts, iou).
297	
298	    Returns ``{slug, schema_version, iou, candidates:[{start,end,shuttle:{5 names}}],
299	    control_segments:[[s,e]], score:{...}, gt_hash}``. CONTROL keeps every candidate then
300	    merges overlaps; NO numpy is touched.
301	    """
302	    fdir = _resolve_dir(slug_or_dir)
303	    slug = fdir.name
304	    traj_csv = fdir / "trajectory.csv"
305	    labels_csv = fdir / "labels.csv"
306	    prov = _read_json(fdir / "provenance.json") if (fdir / "provenance.json").is_file() else None
307	
308	    fps, fw = _fps_fw(prov, slug)
309	    iou = float(prov["iou"]) if (prov and "iou" in prov) else 0.5
310	    proposal = _pinned_proposal(prov)
311	
312	    intervals, feature_rows = distill_local.build_candidates(
313	        str(traj_csv), fps=fps, frame_width=fw, proposal=proposal)
314	
315	    # Build VideoCandidates DIRECTLY with ONLY the 5 shuttle feature columns. We never call
316	    # fusion_compare.load_videos / ablation.load_videos — those read c['person'] unconditionally
317	    # and would KeyError on these person-free fixtures (HARD CONSTRAINT 1).
318	    features: Dict[str, List[float]] = {
319	        name: [row[i] for row in feature_rows] for i, name in enumerate(SHUTTLE_FEATURES)
320	    }
321	    gts = load_gt_intervals(str(labels_csv), strict=True)
322	    vc = VideoCandidates(name=slug, intervals=intervals, features=features, gts=gts)
323	
324	    preds = predict(CONTROL, vc)  # CONTROL: keep all → merge_overlaps; pure-stdlib, no numpy
325	    sc = score(preds, gts, iou)
326	
327	    candidates: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
328	    for j, (s, e) in enumerate(intervals):
329	        shuttle = {name: _round(feature_rows[j][i]) for i, name in enumerate(SHUTTLE_FEATURES)}
330	        # net_crossings is conceptually an integer count — keep it exact (int), per constraint 2.
331	        shuttle["net_crossings"] = int(round(feature_rows[j][SHUTTLE_FEATURES.index("net_crossings")]))
332	        candidates.append({"start": _round(s), "end": _round(e), "shuttle": shuttle})
333	
334	    return {
335	        "slug": slug,
336	        "schema_version": CURRENT_SCHEMA,
337	        "iou": _round(iou),
338	        "candidates": candidates,
339	        "control_segments": [[_round(s), _round(e)] for s, e in preds],
340	        "score": {
341	            "iou_threshold": _round(sc.iou_threshold),
342	            "num_pred": int(sc.num_pred),
343	            "num_gt": int(sc.num_gt),
344	            "true_positives": int(sc.true_positives),
345	            "false_positives": int(sc.false_positives),
346	            "false_negatives": int(sc.false_negatives),
347	            "precision": _round(sc.precision),
348	            "recall": _round(sc.recall),
349	            "f1": _round(sc.f1),
350	            "mean_iou": _round(sc.mean_iou),
351	            "mean_start_error": _round(sc.mean_start_error),
352	            "mean_end_error": _round(sc.mean_end_error),
353	        },
354	        "gt_hash": gt_hash(gts),
355	    }
356	
357	
358	# ======================================================================================
359	# Freeze / refresh (expected.json + provenance.json are correct-by-construction)
360	# ======================================================================================
361	
362	
363	def _provenance(spec_or_dir: Any, slug: str, fps: float, fw: float, iou: float = 0.5
364	                ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
365	    """The committed provenance block — pins the windowing + privacy/licensing tags (§4c, §5)."""
366	    desc = ""
367	    if isinstance(spec_or_dir, FixtureSpec):
368	        desc = spec_or_dir.description
369	    return {
370	        "slug": slug,
371	        "schema_version": CURRENT_SCHEMA,
372	        "kind": "synthetic",
373	        "license": "synthetic",
374	        "minors_free": True,
375	        "description": desc,
376	        "fps": _round(fps),
377	        "frame_width": _round(fw),
378	        "iou": _round(iou),
379	        "windowing": {
380	            "vt": PINNED_VT,
381	            "mg": PINNED_MERGE_GAP,
382	            "mwd": PINNED_MIN_WINDOW,
383	            "max_win": PINNED_MAX_WINDOW,
384	        },
385	        "paths": {
386	            "trajectory": "trajectory.csv",
387	            "labels": "labels.csv",
388	            "expected": "expected.json",
389	        },
390	        "note": (
391	            "Synthetic, deterministic, owned (no real footage). Tier-0 / CONTROL-only. "
392	            "Plumbing-correctness fixture — NOT a measure of rally-detection quality (§6)."
393	        ),
394	    }
395	
396	
397	def freeze_fixture(slug: str, fixtures_dir: Path = FIXTURES_DIR,
398	                   iou: float = 0.5) -> Dict[str, Any]:
399	    """Compute ``expected.json`` + ``provenance.json`` for one fixture from its COMMITTED
400	    ``trajectory.csv`` + ``labels.csv`` via the replay (so expected is correct-by-construction).
401	
402	    Idempotent: rewrites the same bytes given the same committed inputs. Requires the trajectory
403	    + labels CSVs to already exist (use ``--freeze-synthetic`` to generate them first).
404	    """
405	    fdir = fixtures_dir / slug
406	    traj_csv, labels_csv = fdir / "trajectory.csv", fdir / "labels.csv"
407	    if not traj_csv.is_file() or not labels_csv.is_file():
408	        raise FileNotFoundError(
409	            f"{slug}: trajectory.csv/labels.csv missing — run --freeze-synthetic to generate them")
410	
411	    # Provenance first (it pins the windowing the replay reads), then replay → expected.
412	    spec = _spec_by_slug().get(slug)
413	    fps, fw = (spec.fps, spec.frame_width) if spec is not None else _fps_fw_from_prov(fdir, slug)
414	    prov = _provenance(spec if spec is not None else fdir, slug, fps, fw, iou)
415	    _write_json(fdir / "provenance.json", prov)
416	
417	    expected = replay_fixture(fdir)
418	    _write_json(fdir / "expected.json", expected)
419	    return expected
420	
421	
422	def freeze_synthetic(fixtures_dir: Path = FIXTURES_DIR) -> List[str]:
423	    """Generate trajectory.csv + labels.csv for every built-in scenario, freeze expected.json +
424	    provenance.json, and (re)write the manifest. Returns the slugs frozen."""
425	    specs = builtin_specs()
426	    fixtures_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
427	    slugs: List[str] = []
428	    for spec in specs:
429	        fdir = fixtures_dir / spec.slug
430	        fdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
431	        rows = make_synthetic_trajectory(spec)
432	        write_trajectory_csv(rows, fdir / "trajectory.csv")
433	        write_labels_csv(spec.gts, fdir / "labels.csv")
434	        freeze_fixture(spec.slug, fixtures_dir=fixtures_dir)
435	        slugs.append(spec.slug)
436	    _write_manifest(specs, fixtures_dir)
437	    return slugs
438	
439	
440	def refresh_snapshot(slug: Optional[str] = None, all_slugs: bool = False,
441	                     fixtures_dir: Path = FIXTURES_DIR) -> List[str]:
442	    """Re-freeze expected.json (+ provenance.json) from the COMMITTED trajectory.csv + labels.csv —
443	    NO regeneration of the trajectory/labels. ⚠ §6: this can rubber-stamp a real regression; the
444	    mitigation is reviewing the visible diff + recording a reason (the CLI requires one)."""
445	    if all_slugs:
446	        targets = [d.name for d in sorted(fixtures_dir.iterdir())
447	                   if d.is_dir() and (d / "trajectory.csv").is_file()]
448	    elif slug:
449	        targets = [slug]
450	    else:
451	        raise ValueError("refresh_snapshot needs --slug S or --all")
452	    for s in targets:
453	        freeze_fixture(s, fixtures_dir=fixtures_dir)
454	    return targets
455	
456	
457	# ======================================================================================
458	# Manifest + loaders
459	# ======================================================================================
460	
461	
462	def _write_manifest(specs: Sequence[FixtureSpec], fixtures_dir: Path) -> None:
463	    entries = [{
464	        "slug": spec.slug,
465	        "fps": _round(spec.fps),
466	        "frame_width": _round(spec.frame_width),
467	        "windowing": {
468	            "vt": PINNED_VT, "mg": PINNED_MERGE_GAP,
469	            "mwd": PINNED_MIN_WINDOW, "max_win": PINNED_MAX_WINDOW,
470	        },
471	        "kind": "synthetic",
472	        "schema_version": CURRENT_SCHEMA,
473	        "paths": {
474	            "dir": spec.slug,
475	            "trajectory": f"{spec.slug}/trajectory.csv",
476	            "labels": f"{spec.slug}/labels.csv",
477	            "expected": f"{spec.slug}/expected.json",
478	            "provenance": f"{spec.slug}/provenance.json",
479	        },
480	    } for spec in specs]
481	    _write_json(fixtures_dir / "manifest.json", entries)
482	
483	
484	def load_manifest(fixtures_dir: Path = FIXTURES_DIR) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
485	    """Load the fixtures manifest (returns [] if absent)."""
486	    path = fixtures_dir / "manifest.json"
487	    if not path.is_file():
488	        return []
489	    data = _read_json(path)
490	    if not isinstance(data, list):  # pragma: no cover - corrupt manifest
491	        raise ValueError(f"{path}: manifest must be a JSON array")
492	    return data
493	
494	
495	def load_fixture(slug: str, fixtures_dir: Path = FIXTURES_DIR) -> Dict[str, Any]:
496	    """Load one fixture's committed artifacts: ``{provenance, expected, dir}``. Refuses a fixture
497	    whose ``schema_version`` exceeds ``CURRENT_SCHEMA`` (forward-incompatible)."""
498	    fdir = fixtures_dir / slug
499	    prov = _read_json(fdir / "provenance.json")
500	    expected = _read_json(fdir / "expected.json")
501	    sv = int(expected.get("schema_version", prov.get("schema_version", 0)))
502	    if sv > CURRENT_SCHEMA:
503	        raise ValueError(
504	            f"{slug}: schema_version {sv} > CURRENT_SCHEMA {CURRENT_SCHEMA} — upgrade the harness")
505	    return {"slug": slug, "dir": fdir, "provenance": prov, "expected": expected}
506	
507	
508	# ======================================================================================
509	# Parse-compare (NEVER byte-compare): exact ints/structure, abs-tol floats
510	# ======================================================================================
511	
512	
513	def compare_expected(recomputed: Dict[str, Any], committed: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[str]:
514	    """Return a list of human-readable mismatch messages (empty == match).
515	
516	    Parse-compare per HARD CONSTRAINT 2: structural/int fields (candidate count, net_crossings,
517	    TP/FP/FN, segment count, num_pred/num_gt, slug, schema_version, gt_hash) must be EXACTLY
518	    equal; float fields compared with abs tolerance 1e-6. Never a byte compare — CRLF/float-format
519	    safe (the repo runs with core.autocrlf=true)."""
520	    out: List[str] = []
521	
522	    def exact(path: str, a: Any, b: Any) -> None:
523	        if a != b:
524	            out.append(f"{path}: {a!r} != {b!r} (exact)")
525	
526	    def approx(path: str, a: Any, b: Any) -> None:
527	        try:
528	            if abs(float(a) - float(b)) > _FLOAT_TOL:
529	                out.append(f"{path}: {a!r} != {b!r} (|Δ| > {_FLOAT_TOL})")
530	        except (TypeError, ValueError):
531	            out.append(f"{path}: non-numeric float field {a!r} vs {b!r}")
532	
533	    exact("slug", recomputed.get("slug"), committed.get("slug"))
534	    exact("schema_version", recomputed.get("schema_version"), committed.get("schema_version"))
535	    exact("gt_hash", recomputed.get("gt_hash"), committed.get("gt_hash"))
536	    approx("iou", recomputed.get("iou"), committed.get("iou"))
537	
538	    rc, cc = recomputed.get("candidates", []), committed.get("candidates", [])
539	    if len(rc) != len(cc):
540	        out.append(f"candidates: count {len(rc)} != {len(cc)} (exact)")
541	    else:
542	        for i, (r, c) in enumerate(zip(rc, cc)):
543	            approx(f"candidates[{i}].start", r.get("start"), c.get("start"))
544	            approx(f"candidates[{i}].end", r.get("end"), c.get("end"))
545	            rs, cs = r.get("shuttle", {}), c.get("shuttle", {})
546	            if set(rs) != set(cs):
547	                out.append(f"candidates[{i}].shuttle: keys {sorted(rs)} != {sorted(cs)} (exact)")
548	            else:
549	                for k in rs:
550	                    if k == "net_crossings":
551	                        exact(f"candidates[{i}].shuttle.net_crossings", rs[k], cs[k])
552	                    else:
553	                        approx(f"candidates[{i}].shuttle.{k}", rs[k], cs[k])
554	
555	    rseg, cseg = recomputed.get("control_segments", []), committed.get("control_segments", [])
556	    if len(rseg) != len(cseg):
557	        out.append(f"control_segments: count {len(rseg)} != {len(cseg)} (exact)")
558	    else:
559	        for i, (r, c) in enumerate(zip(rseg, cseg)):
560	            approx(f"control_segments[{i}][0]", r[0], c[0])
561	            approx(f"control_segments[{i}][1]", r[1], c[1])
562	
563	    rsc, csc = recomputed.get("score", {}), committed.get("score", {})
564	    int_fields = ("num_pred", "num_gt", "true_positives", "false_positives", "false_negatives")
565	    float_fields = ("iou_threshold", "precision", "recall", "f1", "mean_iou",
566	                    "mean_start_error", "mean_end_error")
567	    for k in int_fields:
568	        exact(f"score.{k}", rsc.get(k), csc.get(k))
569	    for k in float_fields:
570	        approx(f"score.{k}", rsc.get(k), csc.get(k))
571	
572	    return out
573	
574	
575	# ======================================================================================
576	# Small JSON / path helpers (deterministic writers: sort_keys, LF, trailing newline)
577	# ======================================================================================
578	
579	
580	def _write_json(path: Path, obj: Any) -> None:
581	    text = json.dumps(obj, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)
582	    path.write_text(text + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
583	
584	
585	def _read_json(path: Path) -> Any:
586	    with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
587	        return json.load(f)
588	
589	
590	def _resolve_dir(slug_or_dir: Any) -> Path:
591	    if isinstance(slug_or_dir, Path):
592	        return slug_or_dir
593	    p = Path(str(slug_or_dir))
594	    if p.is_dir():
595	        return p
596	    return FIXTURES_DIR / str(slug_or_dir)
597	
598	
599	def _fps_fw(prov: Optional[Dict[str, Any]], slug: str) -> Tuple[float, float]:
600	    if prov and "fps" in prov and "frame_width" in prov:
601	        return float(prov["fps"]), float(prov["frame_width"])
602	    spec = _spec_by_slug().get(slug)
603	    if spec is not None:
604	        return spec.fps, spec.frame_width
605	    raise ValueError(f"{slug}: no fps/frame_width in provenance and not a built-in spec")
606	
607	
608	def _fps_fw_from_prov(fdir: Path, slug: str) -> Tuple[float, float]:
609	    prov_path = fdir / "provenance.json"
610	    prov = _read_json(prov_path) if prov_path.is_file() else None
611	    return _fps_fw(prov, slug)
612	
613	
614	# ======================================================================================
615	# CLI
616	# ======================================================================================
617	
618	
619	def _cmd_check(fixtures_dir: Path) -> int:
620	    manifest = load_manifest(fixtures_dir)
621	    if not manifest:
622	        print("[golden-fixtures] no fixtures found — run --freeze-synthetic first", file=sys.stderr)
623	        return 1
624	    failures = 0
625	    for entry in manifest:
626	        slug = entry["slug"]
627	        committed = _read_json(fixtures_dir / slug / "expected.json")
628	        recomputed = replay_fixture(fixtures_dir / slug)
629	        mismatches = compare_expected(recomputed, committed)
630	        if mismatches:
631	            failures += 1
632	            print(f"[FAIL] {slug}: {len(mismatches)} mismatch(es) "
633	                  f"(characterization = behaviour-locking, NOT correctness — §6):")
634	            for m in mismatches:
635	                print(f"    - {m}")
636	        else:
637	            sc = recomputed["score"]
638	            print(f"[ok]   {slug}: {len(recomputed['candidates'])} candidates, "
639	                  f"{len(recomputed['control_segments'])} segments, F1={sc['f1']:.6f}")
640	    print(f"[golden-fixtures] {len(manifest) - failures}/{len(manifest)} fixtures match "
641	          f"(synthetic-tier = plumbing correctness, not field quality — §6)")
642	    return 0 if failures == 0 else 1
643	
644	
645	def main(argv: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> int:
646	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(
647	        description="Golden Regression Fixtures (M1) — synthetic, CONTROL-only replay harness.")
648	    g = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
649	    g.add_argument("--freeze-synthetic", action="store_true",
650	                   help="generate synthetic trajectory+labels for all built-in scenarios, then "
651	                        "freeze expected.json+provenance.json + write manifest.json")
652	    g.add_argument("--refresh-snapshot", action="store_true",
653	                   help="re-freeze expected.json from COMMITTED trajectory+labels (no regeneration)")
654	    g.add_argument("--check", action="store_true",
655	                   help="replay all fixtures + parse-compare vs committed expected.json (exit 0/1)")
656	    ap.add_argument("--slug", help="single slug for --refresh-snapshot")
657	    ap.add_argument("--all", action="store_true", help="all slugs for --refresh-snapshot")
658	    ap.add_argument("--reason", help="recorded reason for --refresh-snapshot (review the diff!)")
659	    ap.add_argument("--fixtures-dir", type=Path, default=FIXTURES_DIR,
660	                    help="override the fixtures directory (default: repo eval_baselines/fixtures)")
661	    args = ap.parse_args(list(argv) if argv is not None else None)
662	
663	    fixtures_dir: Path = args.fixtures_dir
664	
665	    if args.freeze_synthetic:
666	        slugs = freeze_synthetic(fixtures_dir)
667	        print(f"[golden-fixtures] froze {len(slugs)} synthetic fixture(s): {', '.join(slugs)}")
668	        print(f"  -> {fixtures_dir}")
669	        return 0
670	
671	    if args.refresh_snapshot:
672	        if not args.slug and not args.all:
673	            ap.error("--refresh-snapshot requires --slug S or --all")
674	        if not args.reason:
675	            print("[golden-fixtures] WARNING: no --reason given. --refresh-snapshot can "
676	                  "rubber-stamp a real regression — review the visible diff (§6).", file=sys.stderr)
677	        targets = refresh_snapshot(slug=args.slug, all_slugs=args.all, fixtures_dir=fixtures_dir)
678	        print(f"[golden-fixtures] refreshed snapshot for: {', '.join(targets)}"
679	              + (f"  (reason: {args.reason})" if args.reason else ""))
680	        return 0
681	
682	    # --check
683	    return _cmd_check(fixtures_dir)
684	
685	
686	if __name__ == "__main__":
687	    raise SystemExit(main())

86	def test_perturbing_trajectory_changes_segments(tmp_path):
87	    """Mutating a copy of a trajectory and replaying yields DIFFERENT segments — proving the
88	    windows are RE-DERIVED from the CSV each run, not merely echoed from expected.json."""
89	    slug = SLUGS[0]
90	    src = gf.FIXTURES_DIR / slug
91	    work = tmp_path / slug
92	    work.mkdir()
93	    # Copy the fixture, then TRUNCATE the trajectory to its first half so the derived window's
94	    # `end` shrinks — a guaranteed structural change in the re-derived segments. (Deleting an
95	    # interior hole would not move start/end, since merge_gap coalesces across it and the frame
96	    # indices of the surviving rows are unchanged.)
97	    lines = (src / "trajectory.csv").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
98	    header, body = lines[0], lines[1:]
99	    mutated = [header] + body[: len(body) // 2]
100	    (work / "trajectory.csv").write_text("\n".join(mutated) + "\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
101	    (work / "labels.csv").write_text((src / "labels.csv").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
102	                                     encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
103	    (work / "provenance.json").write_text((src / "provenance.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
104	                                          encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
105	
106	    original = gf.load_fixture(slug)["expected"]
107	    perturbed = gf.replay_fixture(work)
108	    assert perturbed["control_segments"] != original["control_segments"], (
109	        "perturbed trajectory produced identical segments — replay is echoing, not re-deriving")
110

120	        # Structural (parsed) equality — no float churn, exact same shape/values.
121	        assert gf.compare_expected(refrozen, committed) == [], (
122	            f"{slug}: re-frozen expected.json differs from committed (non-idempotent freeze)")
123	        assert refrozen == committed, f"{slug}: re-frozen expected.json not structurally identical"

=== ci.yml ===
name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [master]
  pull_request:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: ci-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  # Lint gate (ruff.toml at repo root): pyflakes/pycodestyle + bugbear.
  # Also the repo-wide syntax guard — ruff reports invalid-syntax on every
  # tracked .py, including scripts/ and training/, which the backend-only
  # py_compile smoke sweep does not reach.
  lint:
    name: Ruff lint
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: "3.13"
      - name: Install ruff
        run: python -m pip install ruff
      - name: Ruff check
        run: ruff check . --output-format github

  # Type gate (mypy.ini at repo root): lenient green baseline with an explicit
  # per-module quarantine list — ratchet by removing entries as modules clean
  # up. Typed core deps are installed so mypy sees their real signatures
  # (without them ignore_missing_imports would Any-stub half the checks).
  typecheck:
    name: Mypy (lenient baseline)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: "3.13"
      - name: Install mypy + typed core deps
        run: python -m pip install mypy pydantic fastapi uvicorn python-dotenv google-genai numpy
      - name: Mypy
        run: mypy backend training

  # Fast guard against the 7fbbeb0 class of breakage: a SyntaxError or
  # module-level import error shipping while the suite stays green.
  # Deliberately does NOT install cv2/torch/numpy — the smoke tests must hold
  # on machines without the GPU stack (test_import_smoke stubs what's absent).
  import-smoke:
    name: Syntax sweep + import smoke (no GPU stack)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: "3.13"
      - name: Install minimal deps
        run: python -m pip install pytest fastapi uvicorn pydantic python-dotenv google-genai
      - name: Run import smoke tests
        run: python -m pytest tests/test_import_smoke.py -v

  full-suite:
    name: Full test suite (offline, mocked)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: "3.13"
          cache: pip
      - name: System libs for opencv-python
        run: |
          sudo apt-get update
          sudo apt-get install -y libgl1 libglib2.0-0t64 || sudo apt-get install -y libgl1 libglib2.0-0
      - name: Install CPU-only torch (no CUDA wheels on a CPU runner)

+# ======================================================================================
+# P1 — De-attribution + minimization: OWNED real trajectory+labels → committable Tier-0

+# §4c anti-attribution, §5 threat model, §7 row P1, §6 honest limits.)
+# ======================================================================================
+
+
+class RefusalError(ValueError):
+    """The provenance/biometric refusal gate (M2) rejected an unsafe freeze.
+
+    A subclass of ``ValueError`` (so existing ``except ValueError`` paths still catch it) that
+    names *why* the unsafe public-fixture path was refused. The refusal is the code-enforced
+    provenance gate (§4c "provenance hard-fail gate", §7 ⑧): the owner-recorded / minors-excluded
+    / license flags are human-asserted, but the gate makes the unsafe path impossible to reach
+    *silently* — a fixture cannot be written unless all three are affirmatively set.
+    """
+
+
+def _new_surrogate_slug() -> str:
+    """An opaque RANDOM surrogate slug — ``fx_<16 hex>`` from ``os.urandom`` (§4c).
+
+    Deliberately NOT the MD5 ``video_id`` and NOT ``HMAC(salt, video_id‖name)`` — a random,
+    NON-reversible id with no algebraic link to the source, so it can't be brute-forced over a
+    tiny known roster even if a salt leaked. The surrogate→source map (if kept) lives off-git.
+    """
+    return f"fx_{os.urandom(8).hex()}"
+
+
+#: The fixed schema filenames the writers ALWAYS emit (as ``paths`` literals). Source-path tokens
+#: that collide with these are NOT a leak — exclude them so a short source stem like ``traj`` can't
+#: false-positive against the literal ``trajectory.csv``/``labels.csv``/``expected.json``.
+_SCHEMA_FILENAME_LITERALS: Tuple[str, ...] = (
+    "trajectory", "trajectory.csv", "labels", "labels.csv", "expected", "expected.json",
+    "provenance", "provenance.json",
+)
+
+
+def _scan_forbidden(text: str, *, where: str, source_paths: Sequence[str]) -> None:
+    """POSITIVE privacy assertion (§4c, red-team ④): raise if ``text`` leaks anything attributable.
+
+    Scans the written JSON *text* for (a) any ``FORBIDDEN_KEYS`` biometric/identity substring,
+    (b) a 32-hex MD5 (the ``video_id`` shape), or (c) any identifying token of a source path
+    (full path / basename / stem) — excluding the fixed schema filename literals the writers
+    always emit. The trajectory is shuttle-only by nature; this guards against a *regression*
+    re-introducing a person/audio/pose stream or an id/path back-channel — never the silent default.
+    """
+    low = text.lower()
+    for key in FORBIDDEN_KEYS:
+        if key in low:
+            raise RefusalError(
+                f"{where}: forbidden key substring {key!r} found in written output — "
+                f"Tier-0 fixtures are shuttle-only; person/pose/gait/face/audio/player MUST NOT "
+                f"appear (docs/GOLDEN_REGRESSION_FIXTURES.md §4c).")
+    m = _MD5_RE.search(text)
+    if m:
+        raise RefusalError(
+            f"{where}: a 32-hex MD5 ({m.group(0)!r}) found in written output — that is the "
+            f"video_id shape and an attribution back-channel; never write it (§4c/§5).")
+    for sp in source_paths:
+        if not sp:
+            continue
+        p = Path(sp)
+        for token in (sp, p.name, p.stem):
+            t = (token or "").strip().lower()
+            # Skip a token that is itself a substring of a schema-literal the writers always emit
+            # (e.g. a source stem ``traj`` ⊂ ``trajectory.csv``) — that collision is not a leak.
+            if not t or any(t in lit for lit in _SCHEMA_FILENAME_LITERALS):
+                continue
+            if t in low:
+                raise RefusalError(
+                    f"{where}: source-path token {token!r} found in written output — the source "
+                    f"filename/path must never be persisted (§4c metadata strip).")
+
+
+def _reset_trajectory_rows(points: Sequence[Any], t0_frame: int
+                           ) -> List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]]:
+    """Shift every ``TrajectoryPoint`` frame by ``-t0_frame`` → ``(Frame,Visibility,X,Y)`` rows.
+
+    Visibility/X/Y are passed through untouched (the shuttle geometry is metric-invariant under a
+    pure time shift); only the absolute frame index moves toward 0, severing the match timeline.
+    """
+    rows: List[Tuple[int, int, float, float]] = []
+    for p in points:
+        rows.append((int(p.frame) - t0_frame, 1 if p.visible else 0, float(p.x), float(p.y)))
+    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r[0])
+    return rows
+
+
+def _reset_labels(gts: Sequence[Interval], t0_sec: float) -> List[Interval]:
+    """Shift every label by ``-t0_sec`` (same offset as the trajectory → metric-invariant)."""
+    return [(s - t0_sec, e - t0_sec) for s, e in gts]
+
+
+def freeze_real_fixture(
+    label: str,

+) -> Dict[str, Any]:
+    """Turn an OWNED, minors-free real trajectory + golden labels into a committable, de-attributed
+    Tier-0 fixture — reusing M1's ``replay_fixture`` for the snapshot. (§4c / §5 / §7 row P1.)
+
+    The refusal gate (M2) makes the unsafe path impossible to reach silently: a fixture is emitted
+    ONLY when ``minors_free is True`` AND ``owned is True`` AND ``license`` is a non-blank string.
+    The committed bytes carry NO filename / video_id / match / player / capture-date — only an
+    opaque random surrogate slug, the shuttle-only trajectory (time-origin reset by default), the
+    reset labels, and a ``kind="cleared_real"`` provenance block. A positive privacy assertion
+    re-reads the written ``expected.json`` + ``provenance.json`` and refuses if anything attributable
+    slipped in.
+
+    NOTE (§6 honest limits): the random surrogate + reset timeline make the fixture non-attributable
+    only *relative to the unpublished source* — it is NOT absolutely anonymous (EDPS v. SRB). De-
+    attribution does not cure provenance/licensing; this sits BEHIND counsel sign-off (P2) and the
+    owner-asserted Fork-A / minors / biometric-exclusion flags.
+
+    Returns the recomputed ``expected.json`` snapshot dict (identical to ``replay_fixture``).
+    """
+    # --- (a) REFUSAL GATE (M2) — the code-enforced provenance hard-fail (§4c / §7 ⑧) -------
+    reasons: List[str] = []
+    if minors_free is not True:
+        reasons.append("minors_free must be True (DPDP child = under 18; behavioural monitoring "
+                       "of a minor is prohibited — §3, guardrail 'exclude minors')")
+    if owned is not True:
+        reasons.append("owned must be True (only owner-recorded Fork-A source is safe to commit; "
+                       "broadcast/scraped = Fork B, do NOT commit — §3 D1)")
+    if not (isinstance(license, str) and license.strip()):
+        reasons.append("license must be a non-blank string (per-record provenance tag — §4c "
+                       "'provenance hard-fail gate')")
+    if reasons:
+        raise RefusalError(
+            "REFUSED to freeze a real Tier-0 fixture — unsafe provenance (de-attribution does NOT "
+            "cure provenance/licensing; this gate sits behind counsel sign-off, §6/P2):\n  - "
+            + "\n  - ".join(reasons))
+
+    traj_path = Path(str(trajectory_csv_path))
+    labels_path = Path(str(labels_csv_path))
+
+    # --- (b) opaque RANDOM surrogate slug — never the video_id MD5, never the source name ---
+    out_slug = slug if slug is not None else _new_surrogate_slug()
+
+    # --- (c) read via the SHIPPED readers; labels via the STRICT loader (never lenient) ----

+    gts = load_gt_intervals(str(labels_path), strict=True)
+
+    # --- (d) consistent time-origin reset (default ON; MUST be metric-invariant, §4c) ------

+    # score is bit-identical with vs without reset — only the absolute timeline is severed. The
+    # quality-floor / characterization snapshot is therefore unchanged (asserted in the tests).
+    if time_origin_reset:
+        t0_frame = min(int(p.frame) for p in points)
+        t0_sec = t0_frame / float(fps)
+    else:
+        t0_frame, t0_sec = 0, 0.0
+    rows = _reset_trajectory_rows(points, t0_frame)
+    reset_gts = _reset_labels(gts, t0_sec)
+
+    # --- (e) write the de-attributed fixture (REUSE M1 writers + replay_fixture) -----------
+    fdir = fixtures_dir / out_slug
+    fdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    write_trajectory_csv(rows, fdir / "trajectory.csv")
+    write_labels_csv(reset_gts, fdir / "labels.csv")

+    iou = 0.5

+        "kind": "cleared_real",
+        "label": str(label),
+        "license": license,
+        "minors_free": True,
+        "owned": True,
+        "fps": _round(fps),

+def _manifest_entry_for(prov: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:

+            "labels": f"{slug}/labels.csv",

+
+def _append_manifest_entry(prov: Dict[str, Any], fixtures_dir: Path) -> None:
+    """Add/replace one fixture's manifest row WITHOUT clobbering the others (e.g. synthetic)."""
+    entries = load_manifest(fixtures_dir)
+    entries = [e for e in entries if e.get("slug") != prov["slug"]]
+    entries.append(_manifest_entry_for(prov))
+    entries.sort(key=lambda e: str(e.get("slug")))
+    _write_json(fixtures_dir / "manifest.json", entries)

+            print(f"[golden-fixtures] REFUSED (--freeze-real provenance gate):\n{e}", file=sys.stderr)
+            return 2
+        print(f"[golden-fixtures] froze de-attributed REAL fixture '{expected['slug']}' "
+              f"(kind=cleared_real) -> {fixtures_dir / expected['slug']}")
+        print("  §6: behaviour-locking, NOT correctness; non-attributable only relative to the "
+              "UNPUBLISHED source — never 'anonymous'. Sits behind counsel sign-off (P2).")
+        return 0
+
     # --check
     return _cmd_check(fixtures_dir)

### 215. user (2026-06-16T06:12:45.792Z)

1	"""Golden Regression Fixtures (P1 + M2) — de-attribution / minimization + the refusal gate.
2	
3	These tests exercise ``golden_fixtures.freeze_real_fixture`` (the P1 tooling that turns an OWNED,
4	minors-free real trajectory + golden labels into a committable, de-attributed Tier-0 fixture) and
5	its M2 ``--license`` / ``--minors-free`` / ``--owned`` refusal gate.
6	
7	Everything runs on SYNTHETIC input written into ``tmp_path`` — NO real data, NO network, NO GPU.
8	We reuse M1's synthetic generators (``builtin_specs`` / ``make_synthetic_trajectory`` and the
9	``write_trajectory_csv`` / ``write_labels_csv`` writers) to manufacture the "source" CSVs, then
10	prove:
11	  * the refusal gate blocks every unsafe combination (M2);
12	  * a successful freeze is de-attributed (random ``fx_`` slug, no id/name/path, no FORBIDDEN_KEYS);
13	  * the time-origin reset is metric-INVARIANT (score identical with/without; only absolute
14	    ``control_segments`` shift toward 0);
15	  * the freeze round-trips (``replay_fixture`` + ``compare_expected`` == []).
16	
17	§6 honest-limits caveat: a green suite proves only that the deterministic post-trajectory transforms
18	are unchanged for the frozen cases — characterization is behaviour-locking, NOT correctness.
19	"""
20	import pytest
21	
22	from backend.eval import golden_fixtures as gf
23	
24	
25	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
26	# Helpers — manufacture a SYNTHETIC "real" source (trajectory + labels CSV) in tmp_path.
27	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
28	
29	
30	def _write_source(tmp_path, *, frame_offset: int = 0):
31	    """Write a synthetic source trajectory+labels CSV pair into ``tmp_path``.
32	
33	    Reuses M1's first built-in scenario (a clean single rally), optionally shifted by
34	    ``frame_offset`` frames (and the matching label seconds) to model an arbitrary absolute
35	    capture timeline. Returns ``(traj_path, labels_path, fps, frame_width, gts)``.
36	    """
37	    spec = gf.builtin_specs()[0]  # clean_single_rally
38	    fps, fw = spec.fps, spec.frame_width
39	    rows = gf.make_synthetic_trajectory(spec)
40	    if frame_offset:
41	        rows = [(f + frame_offset, v, x, y) for (f, v, x, y) in rows]
42	    gts = list(spec.gts)
43	    if frame_offset:
44	        off_sec = frame_offset / fps
45	        gts = [(s + off_sec, e + off_sec) for (s, e) in gts]
46	
47	    traj = tmp_path / "src_trajectory.csv"
48	    labels = tmp_path / "src_labels.csv"
49	    gf.write_trajectory_csv(rows, traj)
50	    gf.write_labels_csv(gts, labels)
51	    return traj, labels, fps, fw, gts
52	
53	
54	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
55	# M2 — refusal gate
56	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
57	
58	
59	@pytest.mark.parametrize(
60	    "kwargs, why",
61	    [
62	        ({"minors_free": False, "owned": True, "license": "owned"}, "minors_free=False"),
63	        ({"minors_free": True, "owned": False, "license": "owned"}, "owned=False"),
64	        ({"minors_free": True, "owned": True, "license": ""}, "license=''"),
65	        ({"minors_free": True, "owned": True, "license": "   "}, "license=blank"),
66	        ({"minors_free": True, "owned": True, "license": None}, "license=None"),
67	    ],
68	)
69	def test_refusal_gate_blocks_unsafe(tmp_path, kwargs, why):
70	    """freeze_real_fixture refuses (ValueError) unless minors_free AND owned AND a non-blank license.
71	
72	    The refusal is the code-enforced provenance gate (§4c / §7 ⑧): the unsafe public-fixture path
73	    must be impossible to reach silently.
74	    """
75	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path)
76	    with pytest.raises(ValueError) as ei:
77	        gf.freeze_real_fixture(
78	            "scenario", traj, labels,
79	            fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=tmp_path / "fixtures", **kwargs)
80	    # RefusalError is a ValueError subclass; the message must name a reason.
81	    assert isinstance(ei.value, gf.RefusalError), why
82	    assert "REFUSED" in str(ei.value), why
83	    # Nothing was written for a refused freeze.
84	    assert not (tmp_path / "fixtures").exists() or not any((tmp_path / "fixtures").iterdir()), (
85	        f"{why}: a refused freeze must not write any fixture")
86	
87	
88	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
89	# P1 — de-attribution
90	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
91	
92	
93	def test_real_fixture_is_deattributed(tmp_path):
94	    """A successful safe freeze is de-attributed: kind=cleared_real, fx_-prefixed surrogate slug,
95	    no id/name/filename/source-path key, and NO FORBIDDEN_KEYS in expected.json."""
96	    fixtures = tmp_path / "fixtures"
97	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path)
98	    expected = gf.freeze_real_fixture(
99	        "clean_rally", traj, labels,
100	        license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
101	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=fixtures)
102	
103	    slug = expected["slug"]
104	    assert slug.startswith("fx_"), f"surrogate slug must be opaque fx_<hex>, got {slug!r}"
105	
106	    prov = gf._read_json(fixtures / slug / "provenance.json")
107	    assert prov["kind"] == "cleared_real"
108	    assert prov["minors_free"] is True and prov["owned"] is True
109	    assert prov["license"] == "owned"
110	    assert prov["slug"] == slug
111	    # No attribution keys / no source filename anywhere in the provenance.
112	    for banned in ("video_id", "name", "filename", "path", "match", "capture_date"):
113	        assert banned not in prov, f"provenance must not carry an attribution key {banned!r}"
114	    prov_text = (fixtures / slug / "provenance.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
115	    for tok in ("src_trajectory", "src_labels", str(traj).lower(), str(labels).lower()):
116	        assert tok not in prov_text, f"source token {tok!r} leaked into provenance"
117	
118	    # expected.json carries no biometric/identity/non-shuttle stream.
119	    exp_text = (fixtures / slug / "expected.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower()
120	    for forbidden in gf.FORBIDDEN_KEYS:
121	        assert forbidden not in exp_text, f"FORBIDDEN_KEYS leak {forbidden!r} in expected.json"
122	
123	    # The fixture is registered in the manifest with kind=cleared_real.
124	    manifest = gf.load_manifest(fixtures)
125	    rows = [e for e in manifest if e["slug"] == slug]
126	    assert len(rows) == 1 and rows[0]["kind"] == "cleared_real"
127	
128	
129	def test_supplied_slug_is_honored(tmp_path):
130	    """An explicitly supplied slug is used verbatim (the surrogate map is the caller's concern)."""
131	    fixtures = tmp_path / "fixtures"
132	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path)
133	    expected = gf.freeze_real_fixture(
134	        "scenario", traj, labels, slug="fx_custom00",
135	        license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
136	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=fixtures)
137	    assert expected["slug"] == "fx_custom00"
138	    assert (fixtures / "fx_custom00" / "expected.json").is_file()
139	
140	
141	def test_freeze_real_does_not_clobber_existing_manifest(tmp_path):
142	    """Freezing a second real fixture must APPEND to the manifest, not wipe the first."""
143	    fixtures = tmp_path / "fixtures"
144	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path)
145	    e1 = gf.freeze_real_fixture("a", traj, labels, slug="fx_aaaa0001",
146	                                license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
147	                                fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=fixtures)
148	    e2 = gf.freeze_real_fixture("b", traj, labels, slug="fx_bbbb0002",
149	                                license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
150	                                fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=fixtures)
151	    slugs = {e["slug"] for e in gf.load_manifest(fixtures)}
152	    assert {e1["slug"], e2["slug"]} <= slugs
153	
154	
155	def test_positive_privacy_assertion_catches_leak_in_source_note(tmp_path):
156	    """source_note is non-identifying by contract — a path/name in it trips the positive scan."""
157	    fixtures = tmp_path / "fixtures"
158	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path)
159	    with pytest.raises(gf.RefusalError):
160	        gf.freeze_real_fixture(
161	            "scenario", traj, labels, slug="fx_leak0001",
162	            license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
163	            fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=fixtures,
164	            source_note=str(traj))  # leaking the source path → must be refused
165	
166	
167	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
168	# P1 (d) — time-origin reset is metric-invariant
169	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
170	
171	
172	def test_time_origin_reset_is_metric_invariant(tmp_path):
173	    """A large-offset source frozen WITH reset has IDENTICAL score to the SAME source frozen
174	    WITHOUT reset — but its absolute control_segments shift toward 0 (severed timeline)."""
175	    offset_frames = 9000  # +5 minutes at 30fps — a large absolute capture-timeline offset
176	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path, frame_offset=offset_frames)
177	
178	    with_reset = gf.freeze_real_fixture(
179	        "scenario", traj, labels, slug="fx_reset_on",
180	        license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
181	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=tmp_path / "with",
182	        time_origin_reset=True)
183	    without_reset = gf.freeze_real_fixture(
184	        "scenario", traj, labels, slug="fx_reset_off",
185	        license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
186	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=tmp_path / "without",
187	        time_origin_reset=False)
188	
189	    # (1) Score is bit-identical — metrics.score is built from pure differences.
190	    assert with_reset["score"] == without_reset["score"], (
191	        "time-origin reset must be metric-invariant (score built from pure differences)")
192	
193	    # (2) Absolute timeline differs — the reset pulls control_segments toward 0.
194	    seg_on = with_reset["control_segments"]
195	    seg_off = without_reset["control_segments"]
196	    assert len(seg_on) == len(seg_off) and len(seg_on) >= 1
197	    assert seg_on[0][0] < seg_off[0][0], "reset must shift the first segment start toward 0"
198	    # The shift equals the offset in seconds (within rounding).
199	    shift = seg_off[0][0] - seg_on[0][0]
200	    assert abs(shift - offset_frames / fps) < 1e-3, (
201	        f"segment shift {shift} should equal the frame offset in seconds {offset_frames / fps}")
202	    # Reset trajectory starts at frame 0.
203	    first_frame = int((tmp_path / "with" / "fx_reset_on" / "trajectory.csv")
204	                      .read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()[1].split(",")[0])
205	    assert first_frame == 0, "with reset, the trajectory must start at Frame 0"
206	
207	
208	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
209	# P1 (e/f) — round-trip / correct-by-construction
210	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
211	
212	
213	def test_freeze_real_round_trips_check(tmp_path):
214	    """After freeze_real_fixture, replay_fixture + compare_expected vs the written expected.json
215	    returns [] — idempotent / correct-by-construction (the same path M1's --check uses)."""
216	    fixtures = tmp_path / "fixtures"
217	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path)
218	    expected = gf.freeze_real_fixture(
219	        "scenario", traj, labels, slug="fx_rt000001",
220	        license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
221	        fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=fixtures)
222	
223	    fdir = fixtures / expected["slug"]
224	    committed = gf._read_json(fdir / "expected.json")
225	    recomputed = gf.replay_fixture(fdir)
226	    assert gf.compare_expected(recomputed, committed) == [], (
227	        "a just-frozen real fixture must replay-compare clean (correct-by-construction)")
228	
229	
230	def test_freeze_real_uses_strict_label_loader(tmp_path):
231	    """The labels are read through the STRICT gt_loader — a malformed label row fails loudly,
232	    never silently shrinks the GT set."""
233	    fixtures = tmp_path / "fixtures"
234	    traj, labels, fps, fw, _ = _write_source(tmp_path)
235	    # Corrupt one label row (end <= start) — strict loader must raise.
236	    labels.write_text("start,end\n5.0,1.0\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
237	    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
238	        gf.freeze_real_fixture(
239	            "scenario", traj, labels, slug="fx_strict01",
240	            license="owned", minors_free=True, owned=True,
241	            fps=fps, frame_width=fw, fixtures_dir=fixtures)

158:- `@backend/pipeline/detectors/fusion_features.py` — **pure** per-window person-cue feature math (no I/O/torch/cv2; mirrors rally_gate discipline). Inputs: per-frame `{frame_idx:[(tid,bbox)]}` + the in-window shuttle points + optional normalized `court_polygon` + `net=(axis,pos)`. `::point_in_polygon` (ray-cast), `::person_in_court` (foot-point; None polygon=count-all; bad dims=False), `::in_court_counts`, `::players_in_court` (median), `::in_court_ratio`, `::mean_player_motion` (per-track centre displacement; x by width, y by height), `::two_sided_motion` (both net-halves occupied), `::shuttle_player_colocation` (shuttle inside a person box ±`pad_frac` halo over aligned visible frames — **the held-shuttle discriminator**), `::compute_person_features` (aggregator → the 5 `::PERSON_FEATURE_NAMES`). All scale/translation-invariant, graceful to None/empty. Tests: `tests/test_fusion_features.py`.

20	## 0. REPO LAYOUT — where new files go (READ BEFORE committing a new file)
21	
22	Top-level tree and the **placement rule** for each kind of file. When unsure, prefer an existing
23	**registry / extension point** (`⎇`) over a new top-level file.
24	
25	| You're adding… | Put it in… | Notes |
26	|---|---|---|
27	| Backend / runtime code | `backend/<subsystem>/` | `pipeline/{segmenters,detectors,validators}`, `providers/`, `sports/`, `annotations/`, `infrastructure/`, `reporting/`, `stitcher/`, `utils/`, `config/`, `features/`, `api/`. Most additions are **register a class in the relevant `⎇` registry**, not a new module tree. |
28	| **Eval LIBRARY** (importable, pure — metrics, calibration, a scorer, a feature) | `backend/eval/` | Pure core, no side-effects at import; CLI/I-O lives in a separate driver. These are **imported widely** (e.g. `calibrate_wasb` by 8 sites) — they are library, not scripts; do NOT move them out. |
29	| **Standalone run-driver / one-off entry CLI** (has `__main__`, NOT imported by app code) | **`scripts/`** | e.g. `scripts/run_phase3_eval.py`, `scripts/run_process_and_stitch.py`. Run as `python scripts/<name>.py`. If it needs `load_dotenv()`/`sys.path` before importing backend, add `"scripts/<name>.py" = ["E402"]` to `ruff.toml`. |
30	| **Ops / maintenance tool** (not part of the served app — cache GC, batch admin) | `tools/` | Run as `python -m tools.<name>` (it's an importable package). e.g. `tools/cleanup_caches.py`. Keep the destructive decision path pure + unit-tested. |
31	| Training code (model fitting) | `training/` | Behind the CI-enforced import wall (ADR-008) — `backend.main` must not import it. Own `requirements-train.txt`. |
32	| A test | `tests/test_*.py` | pytest auto-discovers; the full-suite lane has a **70% coverage floor**. Pure/offline + mocked (no GPU/WSL/network). |
33	| A doc (design, plan, living log) | `docs/` | Index it in `docs/README.md`. Only **terminal-state** (DONE/REJECTED) work moves to `docs/archives/`. |
34	| A throwaway repro / debug script | `scratch/` | **Gitignored** + excluded from ruff/pytest. Never imported by app code. |
35	| A model artifact (weights + manifest) | `artifacts/<name>/<ver>/` | ADR-008: **manifest committed** (provenance), **weights gitignored**. |
36	| Eval baseline / leaderboard JSON | `eval_baselines/` | Tracked (survives a clean checkout); the no-regression gate reads it. |
37	| Pipeline run outputs (reels, trajectories, DBs, frames) | `output/` | **Gitignored.** Per-video isolation is planned in `PER_VIDEO_OUTPUT_LAYOUT.md`. |
